    "content": "# p2d2: Public-Public Data-DNA\n\n> [English version below](#english-version) | [Deutsche Version](#deutsche-version)\n\n***\n\n<a name=\"deutsche-version\"></a>\n## Deutsche Version\n\n### Leitgedanke\n\nDas Projekt **p2d2 (Public-Public Data-DNA)** verfolgt das Ziel, die offenen Daten der öffentlichen Verwaltung mit den Daten der Öffentlichkeit (z. B. OpenStreetMap) zu verzahnen. So entstehen zwei unabhängige, aber synchrone Datenpools – wie die beiden Stränge einer DNA:\n\n1. Datenpool der Verwaltungen\n2. Datenpool der Öffentlichkeit\n\nBeide sollen gleichwertig, transparent und souverän miteinander interagieren. Dies fördert Teilhabe, Vertrauen, Innovation und Kooperation in der digitalen Infrastruktur von Städten und Kommunen.\n\n***\n\n### Features\n\n- Integration von OpenStreetMap als öffentliche Datenbasis\n- Verwaltungsdaten synchronisieren mit offenen Daten\n- Interaktive Karten mit OpenLayers + GeoServer/WMS\n- TailwindCSS + Astro für schnelle, moderne Weboberfläche\n- Strikte Open Source-Orientierung (GPLv3) – Offen, transparent, erweiterbar\n- Fokus auf Community-Beiträge: Mitmachen statt nur Zuschauen\n\n***\n\n### Installation & Nutzung\n\n#### Voraussetzungen\n\n- Node.js ≥ 20\n- npm, pnpm oder yarn\n- Linux oder macOS empfohlen (getestet unter Arch Linux)\n\n#### Setup\n\n```bash\n# Repository klonen\ngit clone https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2.git\ncd p2d2\n\n# Abhängigkeiten installieren\nnpm install\n\n# Entwicklungsumgebung starten\nnpm run dev\n\n# Live-Zugriff auf lokalen dev-Server\nfirefox http://localhost:4321\n# bzw. Aufruf im Browser: http://localhost:4321\n\n# Produktion bauen\nnpm run build\n\n# Produktion lokal testen\nnpm run preview\n```\n\n\n#### Kollaboration\n\nEs existieren 3 Feature-Branches:\n\n1. `feature/team-de1/setup`\n2. `feature/team-de2/setup`\n3. `feature/team-fv/setup`\n\nDiese lassen sich aus Team-Repos einbinden und per Webhook automatisch deployen lassen:\n\n1. https://f-de1.data-dna.eu\n2. https://f-de2.data-dna.eu\n3. https://f-fv.data-dna.eu\n\nSie werden nach der QS in den `develop-` und danach `main`-Branch gemerged:\n\n- https://dev.data-dna.eu\n- https://www.data-dna.eu\n\nTechnische Details zum Multi-Repo-Multi-Staging siehe `MultiRepo-MultiStaging.md`.\n\nDie aktuelle Produktivinstanz (Branch `main`) wird also unter **https://www.data-dna.eu** bereitgestellt.\n\n---\n\n### Repos, Branching \\& Deployment\n\nEs existieren drei synchron gehaltene Repositories:\n\n- **[origin](https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2.git)** auf OpenCode.de: Zentrales Repo, CI/CD - \n- **[mirror](https://gitlab.opencode.de/unbox-cologne/p2d2/p2d2-mirror.git)** auf OpenCode.de: Spiegel für Versionsverwaltung\n- **[hub](https://github.com/Peter-Koenig/p2d2-hub.git)** auf GitHub: Internationale Zusammenarbeit\n\nFolgende Branches wurden angelegt, Webseiten und Prozessimplementierung stehen aus (Stand 3.9.2025):\n\n- **develop**: Integration neuer Features, automatisch nach `dev.data-dna.eu`\n- **main**: produktionsreifer Code, Deployment auf `www.data-dna.eu`\n- **release**: Staging \\& finale Tests vor Veröffentlichung\n- **feature/team-name**: Neue Features, getrennt nach Teams\n\nSiehe `MERGEPOLICY.md` für Details.\n\n---\n\n### Mitmachen\n\nWir freuen uns über Beiträge jeder Art:\n\n- Fehler melden\n- Neue Features vorschlagen oder implementieren\n- Dokumentation verbessern\n- Daten beitragen oder qualitätssichern\n- UI/UX-Ideen teilen\n\nSiehe `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\n---\n\n### Dokumentation (Doku-Server im Aufbau)\n\n- [Zentrale Dokumentation](https://doc.data-dna.eu): doc.data-dna.eu\n- Deployment-Regeln: `.github/DEPLOYMENTRULES.md`\n- Code Reviews: `.github/CODEREVIEWGUIDE.md`\n- Branch-Merge-Policy: `.github/MERGEPOLICY.md`\n\n---\n\n### Lizenz\n\nDieses Projekt steht unter der **General Public License Version 3 (GPLv3)** – siehe `LICENSE`.\n\n---\n\n### Kontakt \\& Community\n\n- Website: [www.data-dna.eu](https://www.data-dna.eu)\n- Mastodon: [@P2D2@nrw.social](https://nrw.social/@P2D2)\n- Weitere Kanäle siehe Footer auf der Website\n\n---\n\n<a name=\"english-version\"></a>\n\n## English Version\n\n### Core Idea\n\nThe **p2d2 (Public-Public Data-DNA)** project aims to interlink open data from public administrations with data from the public (e.g., OpenStreetMap). This creates two independent yet synchronized data pools – like the two strands of DNA:\n\n1. Administration data pool\n2. Public data pool\n\nBoth should interact equally, transparently, and sovereignly. This promotes participation, trust, innovation, and cooperation in the digital infrastructure of cities and municipalities.\n\n---\n\n### Features\n\n- Integration of OpenStreetMap as a public data foundation\n- Synchronization of administrative data with open data\n- Interactive maps using OpenLayers + GeoServer/WMS\n- TailwindCSS + Astro for fast, modern web interfaces\n- Strict open source orientation (GPLv3) – Open, transparent, extensible\n- Focus on community contributions: Participate instead of just watching\n\n---\n\n### Installation \\& Usage\n\n#### Prerequisites\n\n- Node.js ≥ 20\n- npm, pnpm or yarn\n- Linux or macOS recommended (tested on Arch Linux)\n\n\n#### Setup\n\n```\n# Clone repository\ngit clone https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2.git\ncd p2d2\n\n# Install dependencies\nnpm install\n\n# Start development environment\nnpm run dev\n\n# Live access to local dev server\nfirefox http://localhost:4321\n# or open in browser: http://localhost:4321\n\n# Build for production\nnpm run build\n\n# Test production build locally\nnpm run preview\n```\n\n\n#### Collaboration\n\nThere are 3 feature branches:\n\n1. `feature/team-de1/setup`\n2. `feature/team-de2/setup`\n3. `feature/team-fv/setup`\n\nThese can be integrated from team repos and automatically deployed via webhook:\n\n1. https://f-de1.data-dna.eu\n2. https://f-de2.data-dna.eu\n3. https://f-fv.data-dna.eu\n\nAfter QA, they are merged into the `develop` branch and then into `main`:\n\n- https://dev.data-dna.eu\n- https://www.data-dna.eu\n\nFor technical details on multi-repo multi-staging, see `MultiRepo-MultiStaging.md`.\n\nThe current production instance (branch `main`) is available at **https://www.data-dna.eu**.\n\n### Repos, Branching \\& Deployment\n\nThere are three synchronized repositories:\n\n- **origin** on OpenCode.de: Central repo, CI/CD - https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2.git\n- **mirror** on OpenCode.de: Mirror for version control - https://gitlab.opencode.de/unbox-cologne/p2d2/p2d2-mirror.git\n- **hub** on GitHub: International collaboration - https://github.com/Peter-Koenig/p2d2-hub.git\n\nThe following branches were created, websites and process implementation are pending (as of 3.9.2025):\n\n- **develop**: Integration of new features, automatically to `dev.data-dna.eu`\n- **main**: production-ready code, deployment to `www.data-dna.eu`\n- **release**: Staging \\& final tests before release\n- **feature/team-name**: New features, separated by teams\n\nSee `MERGEPOLICY.md` for details.\n\n***\n\n### Contributing\n\nWe welcome contributions of all kinds:\n\n- Report bugs\n- Suggest or implement new features\n- Improve documentation\n- Contribute or quality-check data\n- Share UI/UX ideas\n\nSee `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\n### Documentation\n\n- Central documentation: https://doc.data-dna.eu\n- Deployment rules: `.github/DEPLOYMENTRULES.md`\n- Code reviews: `.github/CODEREVIEWGUIDE.md`\n- Branch merge policy: `.github/MERGEPOLICY.md`\n\n***\n\n### License\n\nThis project is licensed under the **General Public License Version 3 (GPLv3)** – see `LICENSE`.\n\n***\n\n### Contact \\& Community\n\n- Website: [www.data-dna.eu](https://www.data-dna.eu)\n- Mastodon: [@P2D2@nrw.social](https://nrw.social/@P2D2)\n- More channels see footer on the website\n\n```\n"

    "name": "deployment.md",
    "content": "# p2d2: Multi-Branch Deployment – Technische Dokumentation\n\n> [English version below](#english-version) | [Deutsche Version](#deutsche-version)\n\n---\n\n<a name=\"deutsche-version\"></a>\n## Deutsche Version\n\n## Produktiv-Modell: Multi-Branch mit automatischen Webhooks\n**Gültig ab November 2025**\n\n---\n\n### 1. Architektur-Übersicht\n\n#### 1.1 Multi-Branch-System\n\nDas p2d2-Projekt nutzt ein Multi-Branch-Deployment-System mit 5 gleichzeitig aktiven Umgebungen:\n\n| Branch                     | Domain                 | Port | Service                    | Speicher | Zweck               |\n|----------------------------|------------------------|------|----------------------------|----------|---------------------|\n| `main`                     | www.data-dna.eu        | 3000 | `astro-main`               | 10.2M    | Produktion          |\n| `develop`                  | dev.data-dna.eu        | 3001 | `astro-develop`            | 9.3M     | Test/Integration    |\n| `feature/team-de1/setup`   | f-de1.data-dna.eu      | 3002 | `astro-feature-team-de1`   | 22.0M    | Team DE1            |\n| `feature/team-de2/setup`   | f-de2.data-dna.eu      | 3003 | `astro-feature-team-de2`   | 24.8M    | Team DE2            |\n| `feature/team-fv/setup`    | f-fv.data-dna.eu       | 3004 | `astro-feature-team-fv`    | 24.6M    | Team FV             |\n\n**RAM:** ~91MB von 64GB verfügbar\n\n#### 1.2 Infrastructure Stack\n\n```\n\nRepository Push\n↓\nWebhook-Server (Port 9321)\n↓\ndeploy-branch.sh\n↓\nGit Clone → Branch → Kommunen-Collection Symlink → npm ci → build\n↓\nService Stop/Start → Cleanup (alte Deployments)\n↓\nServices: astro-*\n├─ astro-main (Port 3000)\n├─ astro-develop (Port 3001)\n├─ astro-feature-team-de1 (Port 3002)\n├─ astro-feature-team-de2 (Port 3003)\n└─ astro-feature-team-fv (Port 3004)\n↓\nReverse Proxy (OPNSense)\n├─ www.data-dna.eu → localhost:3000\n├─ dev.data-dna.eu → localhost:3001\n├─ f-de1.data-dna.eu → localhost:3002\n├─ f-de2.data-dna.eu → localhost:3003\n└─ f-fv.data-dna.eu → localhost:3004\n↓\nLet's Encrypt (via Caddy)\n\n```\n\n### 2. Komponenten\n\n#### 2.1 Webhook-Server\n\n**`/home/astro/webhook-server/index.js`**\n\n- Port: `9321`\n- Funktion: Empfängt GitLab-Push-Events und triggert Deployments\n- Konfiguration: Branch → Deploy-Path + Port Mapping\n- Logs: `journalctl -u webhook-server -f`\n\n```javascript\nbranchConfig: {\n  main: { deployPath: \"/var/www/astro/deployments/main\", port: 3000 },\n  develop: { deployPath: \"/var/www/astro/deployments/develop\", port: 3001 },\n  // ... etc\n}\n```\n\n#### 2.2 Deploy-Skript\n\n**`/var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh`**\n\n1. Verzeichnis anlegen\n2. Git Clone (Branch)\n3. Kommunen-Collection als Symlink (`/var/www/astro/shared/src/content/kommunen`)\n4. `.env.production` verlinken + `PORT` überschreiben\n5. `npm ci --omit=dev` + `npm run build`\n6. Service Stop/Restart\n7. Live-Symlink umschalten\n8. Alte Deployments aufräumen (nur 5 neueste behalten)\n```\nsudo -u astro /var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh main /var/www/astro/deployments/main 3000\nsudo -u astro /var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh develop /var/www/astro/deployments/develop 3001\nsudo -u astro /var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh feature/team-de1/setup /var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1 3002\n```\n\n#### 2.3 Systemd Services\n\n**`/etc/systemd/system/astro-*.service`**\n\n```ini\n# astro-main.service\n[Unit]\nDescription=Astro p2d2 - main branch\nAfter=network.target\n\n[Service]\nType=simple\nUser=astro\nWorkingDirectory=/var/www/astro/deployments/main/live\nEnvironment=\"PORT=3000\"\nExecStart=/usr/bin/node /var/www/astro/deployments/main/live/dist/server/entry.mjs\nRestart=on-failure\nRestartSec=10\n\n[Install]\nWantedBy=multi-user.target\n```\n\n#### 2.4 Caddy Reverse Proxy (OPNSense)\n\nIn OPNSense: **Dienste → Caddy → Reverse Proxy**\n\n| Domain | Handler | Upstream |\n| :-- | :-- | :-- |\n| https://www.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3000 |\n| https://dev.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3001 |\n| https://f-de1.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3002 |\n| https://f-de2.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3003 |\n| https://f-fv.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3004 |\n| https://opn.data-dna.eu | redir | https://www.data-dna.eu{uri} (301) |\n\n---\n\n### 3. Externe Ressourcen\n\n#### 3.1 Kommunen-Collection\n\n**Quelle:** `/var/www/astro/shared/src/content/kommunen`\nJedes Deployment linkt hierher:\n\n```\n# Symlink\n/var/www/astro/deployments/main/live/src/content/kommunen → /var/www/astro/shared/src/content/kommunen\n/var/www/astro/deployments/develop/live/src/content/kommunen → /var/www/astro/shared/src/content/kommunen\n# ... etc\n```\n\nContent-Updates ohne Deployment möglich!\n\n### 4. Deployment-Verzeichnisstruktur\n\n```\n/var/www/astro\n├── deployments\n│   ├── main\n│   │   ├── deploys\n│   │   │   ├── 20251104003111   ← Latest (Clone + Build)\n│   │   │   ├── 20251104002000\n│   │   │   └── ...\n│   │   └── live → deploys/20251104003111   ← Active\n│   ├── develop\n│   │   ├── deploys\n│   │   └── live → ...\n│   ├── feature-de1\n│   ├── feature-de2\n│   └── feature-fv\n├── logs\n│   └── Build-Logs\n└── shared\n    └── src\n        └── content\n            └── kommunen   ← Externe Collection\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. Workflow: Push → Deployment\n\n1. **Developer pusht zu GitLab Branch**:\n\n```\ngit push origin develop\n```   ```\n\n```\n\n2. **GitLab triggert Webhook**:\n\n```\nPOST http://192.168.xxx.yyy:9321/webhook\nHeader: x-gitlab-token: SECRET\nPayload: { ref: \"refs/heads/develop\", ... }\n```\n\n3. **Webhook-Server**:\n    - Extrahiert Branch-Name (`develop`)\n    - Schlägt `branchConfig` nach (findet `astro-develop` Service)\n    - Triggert: `deploy-branch.sh develop /var/www/astro/deployments/develop 3001`\n4. **Deploy-Skript**:\n    - Erstellt Verzeichnis: `20251104HHMMSS`\n    - Klont `develop`-Branch\n    - Linkt Kommunen-Collection\n    - Buildet Astro-App\n    - Stoppt `astro-develop` Service\n    - Switcht Symlink\n    - Startet `astro-develop` Service\n5. **Service startet neuen Build**:\n    - Node.js lädt neue Version\n    - App läuft auf Port 3001\n6. **Caddy routet Traffic**:\n    - `dev.data-dna.eu` → `localhost:3001`\n    - Neue Version live!\n\n---\n\n### 6. Sicherheit \\& Zugriffskontrolle\n\n#### 6.1 Webhook-Token\n\nToken in `.env.production`:\n\n```\n# /home/astro/.env.production\ngrep SECRET_TOKEN\n```\n\n- Muss zwischen GitLab und Server identisch sein\n- Sollte regelmäßig rotiert werden\n\n#### 6.2 Sudo-Berechtigungen\n\nKonfiguration: `/etc/sudoers.d/astro-systemctl`\n\n```\n# ALLOW: systemctl start/stop/restart astro-*\nALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD: /usr/bin/systemctl start astro-*\nALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD: /usr/bin/systemctl stop astro-*\nALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD: /usr/bin/systemctl restart astro-*\n```\n\n#### 6.3 SSL/TLS\n\n- Automatisch via Let's Encrypt durch Caddy\n- Wildcard-Zertifikat für `*.data-dna.eu` wird automatisch aktualisiert\n\n---\n\n### 7. Monitoring \\& Troubleshooting\n\n#### 7.1 Service-Status\n\n```\n# Alle Services\nsystemctl status astro-*\n\n# Einzeln\nsystemctl status astro-main\nsystemctl status astro-develop\n```\n\n#### 7.2 Logs\n\n```bash\n# Service-Logs\njournalctl -u astro-main -f  # main branch\njournalctl -u astro-develop -f  # develop branch\njournalctl -u webhook-server -f  # Webhook\n\n# Build-Logs\n/var/www/astro/deployments/develop/logs/npm-build-*.log\n/var/www/astro/deployments/develop/logs/clone-*.log\n```\n\n#### 7.3 Build-Status prüfen\n\n```\n# Letzter Build\nls -lt /var/www/astro/deployments/develop/deploys | head -1\n\n# Symlink überprüfen\nls -la /var/www/astro/deployments/develop/live\n\n# App antwortet?\ncurl -I http://localhost:3001\n```\n\n#### 7.4 Häufige Probleme\n\n**Service geht down nach Deployment:**\n\n```bash\n# Logs schauen\njournalctl -u astro-main -n 50\n\n# Working Directory existiert?\nls -la /var/www/astro/deployments/main/live/dist/server\n\n# Port kann gebunden werden?\nlsof -i :3000\n```\n\n**Webhook triggert nicht:**\n\n```\n# Webhook-Server läuft?\nsystemctl status webhook-server\n\n# Port antwortet?\ncurl http://localhost:9321\n\n# Token korrekt?\n/home/astro/.env.production | grep SECRET_TOKEN\n```\n\n---\n\n### 8. Speicherverwaltung\n\n- VM-Disk: 25GB\n- Pro Deployment: ~500MB\n- Aufbewahrung: 5 neueste pro Branch\n- Berechnet: 5 Branches × 5 Deployments × 500MB = 12.5GB → OK mit Reserve\n\n**Cleanup manuell:**\n\n```\n# Alte Deployments für develop\ncd /var/www/astro/deployments/develop/deploys\nls -dt */ | tail -n +4 | xargs rm -rf\n\n# Für alle Branches\nfor branch in main develop feature-de1 feature-de2 feature-fv; do\n  cd /var/www/astro/deployments/$branch/deploys\n  ls -dt */ | tail  ls -dt */ | tail -n +4 | xargs rm -rf 2>/dev/null\ndone\n\n# Diskplatz prüfen\ndf -h\n```\n\n### 9. Wartung \\& Updates\n\n#### 9.1 Regelmäßig\n\n- Logs archivieren (wöchentlich)\n- Alte Deployments aufräumen\n- Webhook-Token rotieren (vierteljährlich)\n- Zertifikate überprüfen\n\n#### 9.2 Bei Problemen\n\n1. Logs überprüfen\n2. Service neustarten\n3. Manuelles Deployment testen\n4. System-Ressourcen prüfen (RAM, Disk)\n\n### 10. Referenzen\n\n- AstroJS: https://docs.astro.build/en/guides/deploy\n- Caddy: https://caddyserver.com/docs/quick-start\n- GitLab Webhooks: https://docs.gitlab.com/user/project/integrations/webhooks\n- systemd: https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/systemd.service.html\n\n<a name=\"english-version\"></a>\n\n## English Version\n\n## Production Model: Multi-Branch with Automatic Webhooks\n\n**Valid as of November 2025**\n\n### 1. Architecture Overview\n\n#### 1.1 Multi-Branch System\n\nThe p2d2 project uses a multi-branch deployment system with 5 simultaneously active environments:\n\n| Branch | Domain | Port | Service | Storage | Purpose |\n| :-- | :-- | :-- | :-- | :-- | :-- |\n| `main` | www.data-dna.eu | 3000 | `astro-main` | 10.2M | Production |\n| `develop` | dev.data-dna.eu | 3001 | `astro-develop` | 9.3M | Test/Integration |\n| `feature/team-de1/setup` | f-de1.data-dna.eu | 3002 | `astro-feature-team-de1` | 22.0M | Team DE1 |\n| `feature/team-de2/setup` | f-de2.data-dna.eu | 3003 | `astro-feature-team-de2` | 24.8M | Team DE2 |\n| `feature/team-fv/setup` | f-fv.data-dna.eu | 3004 | `astro-feature-team-fv` | 24.6M | Team FV |\n\n**RAM:** ~91MB of 64GB available\n\n#### 1.2 Infrastructure Stack\n\n```\nRepository Push\n    ↓\nWebhook Server (Port 9321)\n    ↓\ndeploy-branch.sh\n    ↓\nGit Clone → Branch → Kommunen Collection Symlink → npm ci → build\n    ↓\nService Stop/Start → Cleanup (old deployments)\n    ↓\nServices: astro-*\n├─ astro-main (Port 3000)\n├─ astro-develop (Port 3001)\n├─ astro-feature-team-de1 (Port 3002)\n├─ astro-feature-team-de2 (Port 3003)\n└─ astro-feature-team-fv (Port 3004)\n    ↓\nReverse Proxy (OPNSense)\n├─ www.data-dna.eu → localhost:3000\n├─ dev.data-dna.eu → localhost:3001\n├─ f-de1.data-dna.eu → localhost:3002\n├─ f-de2.data-dna.eu → localhost:3003\n└─ f-fv.data-dna.eu → localhost:3004\n    ↓\nLet's Encrypt (via Caddy)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. Components\n\n#### 2.1 Webhook Server\n\n**`/home/astro/webhook-server/index.js`**\n\n- Port: `9321`\n- Function: Receives GitLab push events and triggers deployments\n- Configuration: Branch → Deploy-Path + Port Mapping\n- Logs: `journalctl -u webhook-server -f`\n\n```\nbranchConfig: {\n  main: { deployPath: \"/var/www/astro/deployments/main\", port: 3000 },\n  develop: { deployPath: \"/var/www/astro/deployments/develop\", port: 3001 },\n  // ... etc\n}\n```\n\n#### 2.2 Deploy Script\n\n**`/var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh`**\n\n1. Create directory\n2. Git Clone (Branch)\n3. Kommunen Collection as symlink (`/var/www/astro/shared/src/content/kommunen`)\n4. Link `.env.production` + override `PORT`\n5. `npm ci --omit=dev` + `npm run build`\n6. Service Stop/Restart\n7. Switch live symlink\n8. Clean up old deployments (keep only 5 newest)\n```bash\nsudo -u astro /var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh main /var/www/astro/deployments/main 3000\nsudo -u astro /var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh develop /var/www/astro/deployments/develop 3001\nsudo -u astro /var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh feature/team-de1/setup /var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1 3002\n```\n\n#### 2.3 Systemd Services\n\n**`/etc/systemd/system/astro-*.service`**\n\n```\n# astro-main.service\n[Unit]\nDescription=Astro p2d2 - main branch\nAfter=network.target\n\n[Service]\nType=simple\nUser=astro\nWorkingDirectory=/var/www/astro/deployments/main/live\nEnvironment=\"PORT=3000\"\nExecStart=/usr/bin/node /var/www/astro/deployments/main/live/dist/server/entry.mjs\nRestart=on-failure\nRestartSec=10\n\n[Install]\nWantedBy=multi-user.target\n```\n\n#### 2.4 Caddy Reverse Proxy (OPNSense)\n\nIn OPNSense: **Services → Caddy → Reverse Proxy**\n\n| Domain | Handler | Upstream |\n| :-- | :-- | :-- |\n| https://www.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3000 |\n| https://dev.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3001 |\n| https://f-de1.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3002 |\n| https://f-de2.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3003 |\n| https://f-fv.data-dna.eu | Reverse Proxy | http://192.168.xxx.yyy:3004 |\n| https://opn.data-dna.eu | redir | https://www.data-dna.eu{uri} (301) |\n\n### 3. External Resources\n\n#### 3.1 Kommunen Collection\n\n**Source:** `/var/www/astro/shared/src/content/kommunen`\nEach deployment links here:\n\n```bash\n# Symlink\n/var/www/astro/deployments/main/live/src/content/kommunen → /var/www/astro/shared/src/content/kommunen\n/var/www/astro/deployments/develop/live/src/content/kommunen → /var/www/astro/shared/src/content/kommunen\n# ... etc\n```\n\nContent updates possible without deployment!\n\n---\n\n### 4. Deployment Directory Structure\n\n```\n/var/www/astro\n├── deployments\n│   ├── main\n│   │   ├── deploys\n│   │   │   ├── 20251104003111   ← Latest (Clone + Build)\n│   │   │   ├── 20251104002000\n│   │   │   └── ...\n│   │   └── live → deploys/20251104003111   ← Active\n│   ├── develop\n│   │   ├── deploys\n│   │   └── live → ...\n│   ├── feature-de1\n│   ├── feature-de2\n│   └── feature-fv\n├── logs\n│   └── Build Logs\n└── shared\n    └── src\n        └── content\n            └── kommunen   ← External Collection\n```\n\n### 5. Workflow: Push → Deployment\n\n1. **Developer pushes to GitLab branch**:\n\n```bash\ngit push origin develop\n```\n\n2. **GitLab triggers webhook**:\n\n```\nPOST http://192.168.xxx.yyy:9321/webhook\nHeader: x-gitlab-token: SECRET\nPayload: { ref: \"refs/heads/develop\", ... }\n```   ```\n\n```\n\n3. **Webhook Server**:\n    - Extracts branch name (`develop`)\n    - Looks up `branchConfig` (finds `astro-develop` service)\n    - Triggers: `deploy-branch.sh develop /var/www/astro/deployments/develop 3001`\n4. **Deploy Script**:\n    - Creates directory: `20251104HHMMSS`\n    - Clones `develop` branch\n    - Links Kommunen Collection\n    - Builds Astro app\n    - Stops `astro-develop` service\n    - Switches symlink\n    - Starts `astro-develop` service\n5. **Service starts new build**:\n    - Node.js loads new version\n    - App runs on port 3001\n6. **Caddy routes traffic**:\n    - `dev.data-dna.eu` → `localhost:3001`\n    - New version live!\n\n### 6. Security \\& Access Control\n\n#### 6.1 Webhook Token\n\nToken in `.env.production`:\n\n```bash\n# /home/astro/.env.production\ngrep SECRET_TOKEN\n```\n\n- Must be identical between GitLab and server\n- Should be rotated regularly\n\n#### 6.2 Sudo Permissions\n\nConfiguration: `/etc/sudoers.d/astro-systemctl`\n\n```\n# ALLOW: systemctl start/stop/restart astro-*\nALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD: /usr/bin/systemctl start astro-*\nALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD: /usr/bin/systemctl stop astro-*\nALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD: /usr/bin/systemctl restart astro-*\n```\n\n#### 6.3 SSL/TLS\n\n- Automatic via Let's Encrypt through Caddy\n- Wildcard certificate for `*.data-dna.eu` is automatically renewed\n\n### 7. Monitoring \\& Troubleshooting\n\n#### 7.1 Service Status\n\n```bash\n# All services\nsystemctl status astro-*\n\n# Individual\nsystemctl status astro-main\nsystemctl status astro-develop\n```\n\n#### 7.2 Logs\n\n```\n# Service logs\njournalctl -u astro-main -f  # main branch\njournalctl -u astro-develop -f  # develop branch\njournalctl -u webhook-server -f  # Webhook\n\n# Build logs\n/var/www/astro/deployments/develop/logs/npm-build-*.log\n/var/www/astro/deployments/develop/logs/clone-*.log\n```\n\n#### 7.3 Check Build Status\n\n```bash\n# Latest build\nls -lt /var/www/astro/deployments/develop/deploys | head -1\n\n# Check symlink\nls -la /var/www/astro/deployments/develop/live\n\n# App responding?\ncurl -I http://localhost:3001\n```\n\n#### 7.4 Common Problems\n\n**Service goes down after deployment:**\n\n```\n# Check logs\njournalctl -u astro-main -n 50\n\n# Working directory exists?\nls -la /var/www/astro/deployments/main/live/dist/server\n\n# Port can be bound?\nlsof -i :3000\n```\n\n**Webhook not triggering:**\n\n```bash\n# Webhook server running?\nsystemctl status webhook-server\n\n# Port responding?\ncurl http://localhost:9321\n\n# Token correct?\n/home/astro/.env.production | grep SECRET_TOKEN\n```\n\n### 8. Storage Management\n\n- VM Disk: 25GB\n- Per deployment: ~500MB\n- Retention: 5 newest per branch\n- Calculated: 5 branches × 5 deployments × 500MB = 12.5GB → OK with reserve\n\n**Manual cleanup:**\n\n```bash\n# Old deployments for develop\ncd /var/www/astro/deployments/develop/deploys\nls -dt */ | tail -n +4 | xargs rm -rf\n\n# For all branches\nfor branch in main develop feature-de1 feature-de2 feature-fv; do\n  cd /var/www/astro/deployments/$branch/deploys\n  ls -dt */ | tail -n +4 | xargs rm -rf 2>/dev/null\ndone\n\n# Check disk space\ndf -h\n```\n\n---\n\n### 9. Maintenance \\& Updates\n\n#### 9.1 Regular Tasks\n\n- Archive logs (weekly)\n- Clean up old deployments\n- Rotate webhook token (quarterly)\n- Check certificates\n\n#### 9.2 When Problems Occur\n\n1. Check logs\n2. Restart service\n3. Test manual deployment\n4. Check system resources (RAM, disk)\n\n---\n\n### 10. References\n\n- AstroJS: https://docs.astro.build/en/guides/deploy\n- Caddy: https://caddyserver.com/docs/quick-start\n- GitLab Webhooks: https://docs.gitlab.com/user/project/integrations/webhooks\n- systemd: https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/systemd.service.html\n\n\n"

    "name": "MultiRepo-MultiStaging.md",
    "content": "# p2d2 Multi-Repo Deployment – Komplette Dokumentation\n\n**Gültig ab:** November 2025\n\n---\n\n## 1. System-Architektur\n\n### 1.1 Übersicht\n\n```\n┌────────── Push ──────────┐    ┌────── Push ───────┐\n│  GitLab (opencode.de)    │    │ GitHub (3x Team)  │\n│  ├─ main                 │    │ ├─ team-de1       │\n│  └─ develop              │    │ ├─ team-de2       │\n└────────────┬─────────────┘    │ └─ team-fv        │\n             │                  └───────────────────┘\n             │                        │\n             │       Webhook          │\n             ↓                        ↓\n        ┌────────────────────────────────┐\n        │  Frontend VM (Port 9321)       │\n        │  Webhook-Server                │\n        │  ├─ Secret-Validierung         │\n        │  ├─ Repo-Router                │\n        │  ├─ git clone → staging-Server │\n        │  └─ Deploy-Trigger             │\n        └───────────────┬────────────────┘\n                        │\n                        ↓\n        ┌──────── staging server ──────────┐\n        │  systemd Services                │\n        │  ├─ astro-main (Port 3000)       │\n        │  ├─ astro-develop (Port 3001)    │\n        │  ├─ astro-feature-team-de1 (3002)│\n        │  ├─ astro-feature-team-de2 (3003)│\n        │  └─ astro-feature-team-fv (3004) │\n        └───────────────┬──────────────────┘\n                        │\n                        ↓\n        ┌────────── Präsentation ──────────┐\n        │  Caddy Reverse Proxy (OPNSense)  │\n        │  ├─ www.data-dna.eu → :3000      │\n        │  ├─ dev.data-dna.eu → :3001      │\n        │  ├─ f-de1.data-dna.eu → :3002    │\n        │  ├─ f-de2.data-dna.eu → :3003    │\n        │  └─ f-fv.data-dna.eu → :3004     │\n        └──────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 1.2 Deployment-Flows\n\n#### Flow 1: Main/Develop (dein Repo, GitLab)\n\n```\nDu: git push origin develop\n    ↓\nGitLab Webhook → POST http://www.data-dna.eu:9321/webhook\n    ↓\nWebhook-Server:\n├─ Liest x-gitlab-token Header\n├─ Validiert gegen SECRET_DEVELOP\n├─ Findet: https://gitlab.opencode.de/.../p2d2.git\n├─ Branch: develop\n└─ Ruft auf: deploy-branch.sh develop ... https://gitlab...\n    ↓\nDeploy-Script:\n├─ git clone --branch develop\n├─ npm ci + npm run build\n├─ sudo systemctl restart astro-develop\n└─ Live unter dev.data-dna.eu\n```\n\n#### Flow 2: Feature Branches (Team-Repos, GitHub)\n\n```\nTeam: git push origin feature/team-de1/meine-funktion\n    ↓\nGitHub Webhook → POST http://www.data-dna.eu:9321/webhook\n    ↓\nWebhook-Server:\n├─ Liest x-hub-signature-256 Header\n├─ Kalkuliert HMAC-SHA256 mit SECRET_TEAM_DE1\n├─ Validiert Signature\n├─ Branch-Pattern Match: /^feature\\/team-de1\\/.+/ ✓\n├─ Findet: https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git\n└─ Ruft auf: deploy-branch.sh feature/team-de1/meine-funktion ... https://github.com/team-de1/...\n    ↓\nDeploy-Script:\n├─ git clone --branch feature/team-de1/meine-funktion\n├─ npm ci + npm run build\n├─ sudo systemctl restart astro-feature-team-de1\n└─ Live unter f-de1.data-dna.eu\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Webhook-Server Konfiguration\n\n### 2.1 Secrets Management\n\n```\n# /home/astro/webhook-server/.env\n\n# GitLab Secrets (dein Repo)\nSECRET_MAIN=dein_secret_main_hier\nSECRET_DEVELOP=dein_secret_develop_hier\n\n# GitHub Shared Secrets (Team-Repos)\nSECRET_TEAM_DE1=team_de1_secret_hier\nSECRET_TEAM_DE2=team_de2_secret_hier\nSECRET_TEAM_FV=team_fv_secret_hier\n```\n\n### 2.2 Branch-Konfiguration\n\n```\n// /home/astro/webhook-server/index.js (Auszug)\n\nconst branchConfig = {\n  'main': {\n    domain: 'www.data-dna.eu',\n    deployPath: '/var/www/astro/deployments/main',\n    port: 3000,\n    repo: 'https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2.git',\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_MAIN,\n    provider: 'gitlab'\n  },\n\n  'develop': {\n    domain: 'dev.data-dna.eu',\n    deployPath: '/var/www/astro/deployments/develop',\n    port: 3001,\n    repo: 'https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2.git',\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_DEVELOP,\n    provider: 'gitlab'\n  },\n\n  'feature/team-de1': {\n    domain: 'f-de1.data-dna.eu',\n    deployPath: '/var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1',\n    port: 3002,\n    repo: 'https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git',\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_TEAM_DE1,\n    provider: 'github',\n    matchPattern: /^feature\\/team-de1\\/.+/\n  },\n\n  'feature/team-de2': {\n    domain: 'f-de2.data-dna.eu',\n    deployPath: '/var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de2',\n    port: 3003,\n    repo: 'https://github.com/team-de2/p2d2-feature.git',\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_TEAM_DE2,\n    provider: 'github',\n    matchPattern: /^feature\\/team-de2\\/.+/\n  },\n\n  'feature/team-fv': {\n    domain: 'f-fv.data-dna.eu',\n    deployPath: '/var/www/astro/deployments/feature-fv',\n    port: 3004,\n    repo: 'https://github.com/team-fv/p2d2-feature.git',\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_TEAM_FV,\n    provider: 'github',\n    matchPattern: /^feature\\/team-fv\\/.+/\n  }\n};\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Team Onboarding\n\n### 3.1 Schritt 1: Team-Repo erstellen\n\n**Team macht das:**\n\n```\n# Option A: Neues Repo\n# GitHub.com → New Repository → p2d2-feature\n# Clone & Feature-Branch erstellen\ngit clone https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git\ncd p2d2-feature\ngit checkout -b feature/team-de1/setup\n\n# Option B: Fork des Main-Repos\n# GitHub.com → fork Peter-Koenig/p2d2-hub\n# Clone & Feature-Branch erstellen\ngit clone https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git\ncd p2d2-feature\ngit checkout -b feature/team-de1/meine-funktion\n```\n\n### 3.2 Schritt 2: Secret generieren & verteilen\n\n**Du machst das:**\n\n```\n# Secret generieren\nopenssl rand -hex 32\n# Ausgabe: a1b2c3d4e5f6g7h8...\n\n# Mit Team teilen (verschlüsselt!)\n# Signal, PGP, oder sicherer Kanal\n\n# In .env eintragen\necho \"SECRET_TEAM_DE1=a1b2c3d4e5f6g7h8...\" >> /home/astro/webhook-server/.env\n\n# Webhook-Server neu starten\nsudo systemctl restart webhook-server\n```\n\n### 3.3 Schritt 3: GitHub Webhook konfigurieren\n\n**Team macht das:**\n\n1. GitHub → Repository → Settings → Webhooks → Add webhook\n2. **Payload URL:** `http://<deine-ip>:9321/webhook`\n3. **Content type:** `application/json`\n4. **Secret:** Den Secret von dir\n5. **Which events:** `Just the push event`\n6. **Active:** ✅\n7. **Add webhook**\n\n**Test:**\n- Recent Deliveries → Klick auf Eintrag → \"Redeliver\"\n- Oder: Team macht Test-Push zu `feature/team-de1/test`\n\n---\n\n## 4. Development Workflow\n\n### 4.1 Feature entwickeln (Team)\n\n```\n# Team entwickelt lokal\ngit checkout feature/team-de1/neue-funktion\n# ... Code ändern ...\ngit add .\ngit commit -m \"Feature: Neue Funktion\"\ngit push origin feature/team-de1/neue-funktion\n```\n\n**Was passiert automatisch:**\n\n```\nPush → GitHub Webhook\n     → Server validiert Secret\n     → Deploy-Script triggert\n     → f-de1.data-dna.eu updated\n     → LIVE in ~2 Minuten\n```\n\n### 4.2 Feature testen\n\nTeam kann ihre Änderungen live anschauen:\n\n```\n# Team testet unter\nhttps://f-de1.data-dna.eu/\n\n# Bei Änderungen: Einfach neuen Push\ngit add .\ngit commit -m \"Bugfix: xyz\"\ngit push origin feature/team-de1/neue-funktion\n# → Automatisch deployed\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Integration in Main/Develop\n\n### 5.1 Feature Ready → Pull Request\n\n**Team erstellt Pull Request** (in ihrem Repo oder zu deinem):\n\n```\n# GitHub: team-de1/p2d2-feature\n# PR: feature/team-de1/neue-funktion → develop\n\n# Oder: Zu deinem Main-Repo\n# PR: OC000028072444/p2d2\n# feature/team-de1/neue-funktion → develop\n```\n\n### 5.2 Du reviewst & merged\n\n```\n# Du auf deinem Repo\ngit checkout develop\ngit pull origin develop\n\n# Feature-Branch mergen\ngit merge feature/team-de1/neue-funktion\ngit push origin develop\n```\n\n**Was passiert:**\n\n```\nGit Push zu develop\n     ↓\nGitLab Webhook\n     ↓\nServer deployed zu dev.data-dna.eu\n     ↓\nTeam + du können testen auf Staging\n```\n\n### 5.3 Release → Main\n\n```\n# Nach Test/Approval\ngit checkout main\ngit pull origin main\ngit merge develop\ngit push origin main\n```\n\n**Was passiert:**\n\n```\nGit Push zu main\n     ↓\nGitLab Webhook\n     ↓\nServer deployed zu www.data-dna.eu\n     ↓\nLIVE in Produktion!\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Deployment-Verzeichnisstruktur\n\n```\n/var/www/astro/\n├── deployments/\n│   ├── main/\n│   │   ├── deploys/\n│   │   │   ├── 20251104003111/  ← Latest\n│   │   │   ├── 20251104002000/\n│   │   │   └── ...\n│   │   ├── live → deploys/20251104003111/  ← Active\n│   │   └── logs/\n│   ├── develop/\n│   │   ├── deploys/\n│   │   ├── live → ...\n│   │   └── logs/\n│   ├── feature-de1/\n│   │   ├── deploys/\n│   │   ├── live → ...\n│   │   └── logs/\n│   ├── feature-de2/\n│   ├── feature-fv/\n│   └── scripts/\n│       └── deploy-branch.sh\n└── shared/\n    └── src/\n        └── content/\n            └── kommunen/  ← Externe Collection\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Monitoring & Troubleshooting\n\n### 7.1 Logs prüfen\n\n```\n# Webhook-Server\nsudo journalctl -u webhook-server -f\n\n# Deployment-Service\nsudo journalctl -u astro-develop -f\n\n# Build-Logs\ntail -f /var/www/astro/deployments/develop/logs/npm-build-*.log\n```\n\n### 7.2 Häufige Probleme\n\n**Problem: Webhook triggert nicht**\n```\n# 1. Webhook-Server läuft?\nsudo systemctl status webhook-server\n\n# 2. Secret korrekt?\ngrep SECRET_TEAM_DE1 /home/astro/webhook-server/.env\n\n# 3. Logs anschauen\nsudo journalctl -u webhook-server -n 50\n```\n\n**Problem: Build schlägt fehl**\n```\n# Deploy-Logs\ntail -100 /var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1/logs/npm-build-*.log\n\n# Service-Logs\nsudo journalctl -u astro-feature-team-de1 -n 30\n```\n\n**Problem: Service startet nicht**\n```\n# Verzeichnis existiert?\nls -la /var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1/live/\n\n# Port gebunden?\nsudo lsof -i :3002\n\n# Service neu starten\nsudo systemctl restart astro-feature-team-de1\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Sicherheit\n\n### 8.1 Secret-Verwaltung\n\n- ✅ Jedem Team eigener Secret\n- ✅ Secrets in `.env` (nicht im Code)\n- ✅ Datei-Berechtigungen: `600` (nur astro)\n- ✅ GitHub HMAC-SHA256 Validierung\n- ✅ GitLab Token Validierung\n- ⚠️ Secrets regelmäßig rotieren\n\n### 8.2 Branch-Schutz\n\n**Server-seitig: Branch-Pattern-Matching**\n\n```\n// Team DE1 kann NICHT zu feature/team-de2 deployen\n// Server validiert Pattern: /^feature\\/team-de1\\/.+/\n// Unbekannte Branches: 404 (ignoriert)\n```\n\n**GitHub: Branch-Protection-Rules (optional)**\n\n```\nSettings → Branches → Add rule\n├─ Pattern: main\n├─ Require pull request reviews\n└─ Require status checks to pass\n```\n\n---\n\n## 9. Quick Reference\n\n### Secrets generieren\n```\nopenssl rand -hex 32\n```\n\n### Webhook-Server Logs\n```\nsudo journalctl -u webhook-server -f\n```\n\n### Service neu starten\n```\nsudo systemctl restart astro-develop\n```\n\n### Deployment manuell\n```\nsudo -u astro /var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh \\\n  feature/team-de1/setup \\\n  /var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1 \\\n  3002 \\\n  https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git\n```\n\n### Status aller Services\n```\nsudo systemctl status astro-*\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. Zusammenfassung\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Workflow Summary                           │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                             │\n│  Team entwickelt in ihrem GitHub-Repo       │\n│  └─ feature/team-de1/*                      │\n│                                             │\n│  Push triggert Webhook                      │\n│  └─ Server validiert Secret + Branch        │\n│                                             │\n│  Deploy-Script wird aufgerufen              │\n│  └─ Git clone + npm build                   │\n│                                             │\n│  Service wird neu gestartet                 │\n│  └─ f-de1.data-dna.eu LIVE                  │\n│                                             │\n│  Team testet auf Feature-Domain             │\n│  └─ Nach Approval: PR zu develop            │\n│                                             │\n│  Du mergst develop → main                   │\n│  └─ Automatisches Deployment zu www         │\n│                                             │\n│  LIVE in Produktion ✅                      │\n│                                             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n<a name=\"english-version\"></a>\n\n## English Version\n\n**Valid as of November 2025**\n\n***\n\n### 1. System Architecture\n\n#### 1.1 Overview\n\n```\nPush          Push\n  ↓             ↓\nGitLab         GitHub\n(opencode.de)  (3x Team)\n  ↓             ↓\nmain          team-de1\ndevelop       team-de2\n              team-fv\n  ↓             ↓\nWebhook (Frontend VM)\nPort 9321\nWebhook Server\n  ├─ Secret Validation\n  └─ Repo Router\n      ↓\ngit clone\nstaging Server\n  ↓\nDeploy Trigger\n  ↓\nstaging server\nsystemd Services\n  ├─ astro-main (Port 3000)\n  ├─ astro-develop (Port 3001)\n  ├─ astro-feature-team-de1 (3002)\n  ├─ astro-feature-team-de2 (3003)\n  └─ astro-feature-team-fv (3004)\n      ↓\nPresentation\nCaddy Reverse Proxy (OPNSense)\n  ├─ www.data-dna.eu → :3000\n  ├─ dev.data-dna.eu → :3001\n  ├─ f-de1.data-dna.eu → :3002\n  ├─ f-de2.data-dna.eu → :3003\n  └─ f-fv.data-dna.eu → :3004\n```\n\n\n#### 1.2 Deployment Flows\n\n**Flow 1: Main/Develop (your repo, GitLab)**\n\n```\ngit push origin develop\n    ↓\nWebhook → POST http://www.data-dna.eu:9321/webhook\n    ↓\nWebhook Server\n  ├─ Reads x-gitlab-token Header\n  ├─ Validates against SECRET_DEVELOP\n  └─ Finds: https://gitlab.opencode.de/.../p2d2.git\n            Branch: develop\n    ↓\nCalls: deploy-branch.sh develop ... https://gitlab...\n    ↓\nDeploy Script\n  ├─ git clone --branch develop\n  ├─ npm ci\n  ├─ npm run build\n  └─ sudo systemctl restart astro-develop\n    ↓\nLive at: dev.data-dna.eu\n```\n\n**Flow 2: Feature Branches (Team Repos, GitHub)**\n\n```\ngit push origin feature/team-de1/my-function\n    ↓\nWebhook → POST http://www.data-dna.eu:9321/webhook\n    ↓\nWebhook Server\n  ├─ Reads x-hub-signature-256 Header\n  ├─ Calculates HMAC-SHA256 with SECRET_TEAM_DE1\n  ├─ Validates Signature\n  ├─ Branch Pattern Match: feature/team-de1/*\n  └─ Finds: https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git\n    ↓\nCalls: deploy-branch.sh feature/team-de1/my-function ... https://github.com/team-de1/...\n    ↓\nDeploy Script\n  ├─ git clone --branch feature/team-de1/my-function\n  ├─ npm ci\n  ├─ npm run build\n  └─ sudo systemctl restart astro-feature-team-de1\n    ↓\nLive at: f-de1.data-dna.eu\n```\n\n\n---\n\n### 2. Webhook Server Configuration\n\n#### 2.1 Secrets Management\n\n**`/home/astro/webhook-server/.env`**\n\n```\n# GitLab Secrets (your repo)\nSECRET_MAIN=<your-secret-main-here>\nSECRET_DEVELOP=<your-secret-develop-here>\n\n# GitHub Shared Secrets (Team Repos)\nSECRET_TEAM_DE1=<team-de1-secret-here>\nSECRET_TEAM_DE2=<team-de2-secret-here>\nSECRET_TEAM_FV=<team-fv-secret-here>\n```\n\n\n#### 2.2 Branch Configuration\n\n**`/home/astro/webhook-server/index.js`** (excerpt)\n\n```javascript\nbranchConfig: {\n  main: {\n    domain: \"www.data-dna.eu\",\n    deployPath: \"/var/www/astro/deployments/main\",\n    port: 3000,\n    repo: \"https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2.git\",\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_MAIN,\n    provider: \"gitlab\",\n  },\n  develop: {\n    domain: \"dev.data-dna.eu\",\n    deployPath: \"/var/www/astro/deployments/develop\",\n    port: 3001,\n    repo: \"https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2.git\",\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_DEVELOP,\n    provider: \"gitlab\",\n  },\n  \"feature/team-de1\": {\n    domain: \"f-de1.data-dna.eu\",\n    deployPath: \"/var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1\",\n    port: 3002,\n    repo: \"https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git\",\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_TEAM_DE1,\n    provider: \"github\",\n    matchPattern: \"feature/team-de1/*\",\n  },\n  \"feature/team-de2\": {\n    domain: \"f-de2.data-dna.eu\",\n    deployPath: \"/var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de2\",\n    port: 3003,\n    repo: \"https://github.com/team-de2/p2d2-feature.git\",\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_TEAM_DE2,\n    provider: \"github\",\n    matchPattern: \"feature/team-de2/*\",\n  },\n  \"feature/team-fv\": {\n    domain: \"f-fv.data-dna.eu\",\n    deployPath: \"/var/www/astro/deployments/feature-fv\",\n    port: 3004,\n    repo: \"https://github.com/team-fv/p2d2-feature.git\",\n    secret: process.env.SECRET_TEAM_FV,\n    provider: \"github\",\n    matchPattern: \"feature/team-fv/*\",\n  }\n}\n```\n\n\n---\n\n### 3. Team Onboarding\n\n#### 3.1 Step 1: Team creates repo\n\n**Option A: New repo**\n\n```\n# GitHub.com\nNew Repository → p2d2-feature\n\n# Clone\ngit clone https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git\ncd p2d2-feature\n\n# Create feature branch\ngit checkout -b feature/team-de1/setup\n```\n\n**Option B: Fork main repo**\n\n```bash\n# GitHub.com\nfork Peter-Koenig/p2d2-hub\n\n# Clone\ngit clone https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git\ncd p2d2-feature\n\n# Create feature branch\ngit checkout -b feature/team-de1/my-function\n```\n\n\n#### 3.2 Step 2: Generate \\& distribute secret (you do this)\n\n```\n# Generate secret\nopenssl rand -hex 32\n\n# Output:\na1b2c3d4e5f6g7h8...\n\n# Share with team (encrypted! Signal, PGP, or secure channel)\n\n# Add to .env\nSECRET_TEAM_DE1=a1b2c3d4e5f6g7h8...\n# /home/astro/webhook-server/.env\n\n# Restart webhook server\nsystemctl restart webhook-server\n```\n\n\n#### 3.3 Step 3: Configure GitHub webhook (team does this)\n\n1. GitHub Repository → Settings → Webhooks → Add webhook\n2. Payload URL: `http://<your-ip>:9321/webhook`\n3. Content type: `application/json`\n4. Secret: The secret from you\n5. Which events: Just the push event\n6. Active: ✓\n7. Add webhook\n\n**Test:**\n\n- Recent Deliveries → Click on entry → Redeliver\n- Or team makes test push to `feature/team-de1/test`\n\n***\n\n### 4. Development Workflow\n\n#### 4.1 Develop feature (team)\n\nTeam develops locally:\n\n```bash\ngit checkout feature/team-de1/new-function\n\n# ... change code ...\n\ngit add .\ngit commit -m \"Feature: New function\"\ngit push origin feature/team-de1/new-function\n```\n\nWhat happens automatically:\n\n- GitHub → Webhook → Server validates secret\n- Deploy script triggers\n- **f-de1.data-dna.eu** updates → LIVE in ~2 minutes\n\n\n#### 4.2 Test feature\n\nTeam can view their changes live:\n\n- Team tests at **f-de1.data-dna.eu**\n\nFor changes:\n\n```\n# Simply new push\ngit add .\ngit commit -m \"Bugfix: xyz\"\ngit push origin feature/team-de1/new-function\n# → Automatically deployed\n```\n\n\n***\n\n### 5. Integration into Main/Develop\n\n#### 5.1 Feature Ready → Pull Request\n\nTeam creates pull request:\n\n```bash\n# Pull request in their repo\nGitHub: team-de1/p2d2-feature\nPR: feature/team-de1/new-function → develop\n\n# Or: To your main repo\nPR: OC000028072444/p2d2\nfeature/team-de1/new-function → develop\n```\n\n\n#### 5.2 You review \\& merge\n\nYou on your repo:\n\n```\ngit checkout develop\ngit pull origin develop\n\n# Merge feature branch\ngit merge feature/team-de1/new-function\ngit push origin develop\n```\n\nWhat happens:\n\n- Push to `develop` → Webhook deploys to **dev.data-dna.eu**\n- You can test on staging\n\n\n#### 5.3 Release → Main\n\nAfter test/approval:\n\n```bash\ngit checkout main\ngit pull origin main\ngit merge develop\ngit push origin main\n```\n\nWhat happens:\n\n- Push to `main` → Webhook deploys to **www.data-dna.eu**\n- LIVE in production!\n\n---\n\n### 6. Deployment Directory Structure\n\n```\n/var/www/astro\n├── deployments\n│   ├── main\n│   │   ├── deploys\n│   │   │   ├── 20251104003111   ← Latest\n│   │   │   ├── 20251104002000\n│   │   │   └── ...\n│   │   └── live → deploys/20251104003111   ← Active\n│   ├── develop\n│   │   ├── deploys\n│   │   └── live → ...\n│   │   └── logs\n│   ├── feature-de1\n│   │   ├── deploys\n│   │   └── live → ...\n│   │   └── logs\n│   ├── feature-de2\n│   └── feature-fv\n├── scripts\n│   └── deploy-branch.sh\n└── shared\n    └── src\n        └── content\n            └── kommunen   ← External Collection\n```\n\n\n***\n\n### 7. Monitoring \\& Troubleshooting\n\n#### 7.1 Check logs\n\n```bash\n# Webhook server\njournalctl -u webhook-server -f\n\n# Deployment service\njournalctl -u astro-develop -f\n\n# Build logs\ntail -f /var/www/astro/deployments/develop/logs/npm-build-*.log\n```\n\n\n#### 7.2 Common problems\n\n**Webhook not triggering:**\n\n1. Webhook server running?\n\n```\nsystemctl status webhook-server\n```   ```\n\n```\n\n2. Secret correct?\n\n```bash\n# SECRET_TEAM_DE1\n/home/astro/webhook-server/.env\n```\n\n3. Check logs:\n\n```\njournalctl -u webhook-server -n 50\n```   ```\n\n```\n\n\n**Build fails:**\n\n```bash\n# Deploy logs\ntail -100 /var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1/logs/npm-build-*.log\n\n# Service logs\njournalctl -u astro-feature-team-de1 -n 30\n```\n\n**Service doesn't start:**\n\n```\n# Directory exists?\nls -la /var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1/live\n\n# Port bound?\nlsof -i :3002\n\n# Restart service\nsystemctl restart astro-feature-team-de1\n```\n\n\n***\n\n### 8. Security\n\n#### 8.1 Secret Management\n\n- Each team has own secret\n- Secrets in `.env`, not in code\n- File permissions: `600` (only astro)\n- GitHub: HMAC-SHA256 validation\n- GitLab: Token validation\n- Rotate secrets regularly\n\n\n#### 8.2 Branch Protection\n\n**Server-side: Branch Pattern Matching**\n\n- Team DE1 CANNOT deploy to `feature/team-de2`\n- Server validates pattern `feature/team-de1/*`\n- Unknown branches → 404 (ignored)\n\n**Branch Protection Rules (optional):**\n\n```\n# Branches → Add rule\nPattern: main\n✓ Require pull request reviews\n✓ Require status checks to pass\n```\n\n\n---\n\n### 9. Quick Reference\n\n```\n# Generate secrets\nopenssl rand -hex 32\n\n# Webhook server logs\njournalctl -u webhook-server -f\n\n# Restart service\nsystemctl restart astro-develop\n\n# Manual deployment\nsudo -u astro /var/www/astro/scripts/deploy-branch.sh feature/team-de1/setup /var/www/astro/deployments/feature-de1 3002 https://github.com/team-de1/p2d2-feature.git\n\n# Status of all services\nsystemctl status astro-*\n```\n\n\n***\n\n### 10. Summary: Workflow Overview\n\n1. **Team develops** in their GitHub repo (`feature/team-de1`)\n2. **Push** triggers webhook\n3. **Server** validates secret + branch\n4. **Deploy script** is called:\n    - Git clone\n    - npm build\n    - Service is restarted\n5. **f-de1.data-dna.eu** → LIVE\n6. **Team tests** on feature domain\n7. After approval → **PR to develop**\n8. **You merge** → `develop` → `main`\n9. **Automatic deployment** to **www** → LIVE in production\n```\n\n"

    "content": "# Dependency Update Workflow – p2d2 Projekt\n\n## Überblick\n\nDieser Prozess beschreibt das Vorgehen für Updates von Abhängigkeiten (z.B. AstroJS) im p2d2-Projekt mit verteilten Teams und Multi-Repo-Setup.\n\n## Rollen\n\n- **Tech Lead (TL)**: Verantwortlich für Updates und Koordination\n- **Team-Mitglieder**: Testen Updates in ihren Feature-Branches\n\n## Workflow\n\n### Phase 1: Update durch Tech Lead\n\n1. **Feature-Branch erstellen**\n   ```\n   git checkout develop\n   git pull hub develop\n   git checkout -b feature/dependency-update-YYYY-MM-DD\n   ```\n\n2. **Dependencies aktualisieren**\n   ```\n   # Option A: Automatisch (empfohlen für Astro)\n   npx @astrojs/upgrade\n   \n   # Option B: Manuell\n   npm update\n   # oder spezifisch:\n   npm install astro@latest\n   ```\n\n3. **Installation und Test**\n   ```\n   npm install\n   rm -rf .astro/ node_modules/.astro node_modules/.vite\n   DEBUG=astro:* npm run dev -- --host 0.0.0.0\n   ```\n\n4. **Commit und Merge**\n   ```\n   git add package.json package-lock.json\n   git commit -m \"chore: Update dependencies (Astro X.Y.Z)\"\n   git checkout develop\n   git merge feature/dependency-update-YYYY-MM-DD\n   git push all develop\n   ```\n\n### Phase 2: Deployment auf Dev-Umgebung\n\n5. **Automatisches Deployment**\n   - CI/CD deployt `develop` automatisch nach `dev.data-dna.eu`\n   \n6. **Test auf Dev-Umgebung**\n   - TL testet alle Hauptfunktionen auf dev.data-dna.eu\n   - Bei Problemen: Bugfix in `develop` oder Rollback\n\n### Phase 3: Team-Testing\n\n7. **Team-Benachrichtigung**\n   - TL informiert Teams per Issue/Mail/Chat:\n     ```\n     Update verfügbar in develop:\n     - Astro X.Y.Z\n     - [weitere Updates]\n     \n     Bitte pullen, npm install ausführen und testen.\n     Feedback bis [Datum] in Issue #XYZ\n     ```\n\n8. **Team-Members führen lokal aus**\n   ```\n   git checkout develop\n   git pull hub develop\n   npm install\n   rm -rf .astro/ node_modules/.astro node_modules/.vite\n   DEBUG=astro:* npm run dev -- --host 0.0.0.0\n   ```\n\n9. **Feedback-Sammlung**\n   - Teams melden Issues oder geben OK\n   - TL behebt kritische Issues in `develop`\n\n### Phase 4: Production Release\n\n10. **Merge nach Main**\n    ```\n    git checkout main\n    git pull hub main\n    git merge develop\n    git push all main\n    ```\n\n11. **Deployment auf Production**\n    - CI/CD deployt automatisch nach `www.data-dna.eu`\n    - TL führt Smoke-Tests durch\n\n12. **Dokumentation**\n    - Update im CHANGELOG.md dokumentieren\n    - Release-Notes erstellen (optional)\n\n## Wichtige Hinweise\n\n### Für Tech Lead\n\n- **package-lock.json IMMER committen** (garantiert identische Versionen)\n- Breaking Changes im Commit-Message kennzeichnen\n- Bei Major-Updates: ausführliche Tests und Dokumentation\n- `git push all` pusht zu allen Remotes (hub, mirror, origin)\n\n### Für Team-Members\n\n- **Nach jedem Pull mit package.json-Änderungen**: `npm install` ausführen\n- Bei Build-Problemen: Cache löschen (`.astro/`, `node_modules/.astro`, `node_modules/.vite`)\n- Keine Updates in Feature-Branches durchführen (Merge-Konflikte vermeiden)\n\n## Automatisierung (Optional)\n\nGitHub Action für automatische Dependency-Checks:\n\n```\nname: Check Dependencies\non:\n  schedule:\n    - cron: '0 9 * * 1'  # Montags 9:00 Uhr\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\njobs:\n  check-updates:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4\n      - run: npm outdated || true\n      - run: npx @astrojs/upgrade --check\n```\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### \"Module not found\" Fehler nach Pull\n```\nrm -rf node_modules package-lock.json\nnpm install\n```\n\n### Veraltete Remote-Branches\n```\ngit fetch --prune\ngit remote prune origin\n```\n\n### Cache-Probleme\n```\nrm -rf .astro/ node_modules/.astro node_modules/.vite\nnpm run dev\n```\n\n---\n\n# Dependency Update Workflow – p2d2 Project\n\n## Overview\n\nThis process describes the procedure for updating dependencies (e.g., AstroJS) in the p2d2 project with distributed teams and multi-repo setup.\n\n## Roles\n\n- **Tech Lead (TL)**: Responsible for updates and coordination\n- **Team Members**: Test updates in their feature branches\n\n## Workflow\n\n### Phase 1: Update by Tech Lead\n\n1. **Create Feature Branch**\n   ```\n   git checkout develop\n   git pull hub develop\n   git checkout -b feature/dependency-update-YYYY-MM-DD\n   ```\n\n2. **Update Dependencies**\n   ```\n   # Option A: Automatic (recommended for Astro)\n   npx @astrojs/upgrade\n   \n   # Option B: Manual\n   npm update\n   # or specific:\n   npm install astro@latest\n   ```\n\n3. **Install and Test**\n   ```\n   npm install\n   rm -rf .astro/ node_modules/.astro node_modules/.vite\n   DEBUG=astro:* npm run dev -- --host 0.0.0.0\n   ```\n\n4. **Commit and Merge**\n   ```\n   git add package.json package-lock.json\n   git commit -m \"chore: Update dependencies (Astro X.Y.Z)\"\n   git checkout develop\n   git merge feature/dependency-update-YYYY-MM-DD\n   git push all develop\n   ```\n\n### Phase 2: Deployment to Dev Environment\n\n5. **Automatic Deployment**\n   - CI/CD automatically deploys `develop` to `dev.data-dna.eu`\n   \n6. **Test on Dev Environment**\n   - TL tests all main functions on dev.data-dna.eu\n   - If issues occur: Bugfix in `develop` or rollback\n\n### Phase 3: Team Testing\n\n7. **Team Notification**\n   - TL notifies teams via issue/email/chat:\n     ```\n     Update available in develop:\n     - Astro X.Y.Z\n     - [other updates]\n     \n     Please pull, run npm install and test.\n     Feedback until [date] in issue #XYZ\n     ```\n\n8. **Team Members Execute Locally**\n   ```\n   git checkout develop\n   git pull hub develop\n   npm install\n   rm -rf .astro/ node_modules/.astro node_modules/.vite\n   DEBUG=astro:* npm run dev -- --host 0.0.0.0\n   ```\n\n9. **Collect Feedback**\n   - Teams report issues or give OK\n   - TL fixes critical issues in `develop`\n\n### Phase 4: Production Release\n\n10. **Merge to Main**\n    ```\n    git checkout main\n    git pull hub main\n    git merge develop\n    git push all main\n    ```\n\n11. **Deploy to Production**\n    - CI/CD automatically deploys to `www.data-dna.eu`\n    - TL performs smoke tests\n\n12. **Documentation**\n    - Document update in CHANGELOG.md\n    - Create release notes (optional)\n\n## Important Notes\n\n### For Tech Lead\n\n- **ALWAYS commit package-lock.json** (guarantees identical versions)\n- Mark breaking changes in commit message\n- For major updates: extensive testing and documentation\n- `git push all` pushes to all remotes (hub, mirror, origin)\n\n### For Team Members\n\n- **After every pull with package.json changes**: run `npm install`\n- For build issues: clear cache (`.astro/`, `node_modules/.astro`, `node_modules/.vite`)\n- Don't perform updates in feature branches (avoid merge conflicts)\n\n## Automation (Optional)\n\nGitHub Action for automatic dependency checks:\n\n```\nname: Check Dependencies\non:\n  schedule:\n    - cron: '0 9 * * 1'  # Mondays 9:00 AM\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\njobs:\n  check-updates:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4\n      - run: npm outdated || true\n      - run: npx @astrojs/upgrade --check\n```\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### \"Module not found\" errors after pull\n```\nrm -rf node_modules package-lock.json\nnpm install\n```\n\n### Outdated remote branches\n```\ngit fetch --prune\ngit remote prune origin\n```\n\n### Cache issues\n```\nrm -rf .astro/ node_modules/.astro node_modules/.vite\nnpm run dev\n```\n"

    "name": "GDI.md",
    "content": "# Geodateninfrastruktur (GDI) - p2d2 Architektur\n\nStand: 27.10.2025\n- WiP\n- Prototypische Entwicklung\n- aktuell am Beispiel [Friedhof Rheinkassel](https://osm.org/go/0GDpvHY26--?m=)\n\n## Überblick\n\nDie p2d2-Geodateninfrastruktur ist eine standardkonforme, skalierbare GDI-Lösung für die Erfassung, Verwaltung und Bereitstellung von Friedhofsdaten in Kommunen. Die Architektur folgt den Prinzipien der [GDI-DE (Geodateninfrastruktur Deutschland)](https://www.gdi-de.org/) und setzt auf offene Standards des [Open Geospatial Consortium (OGC)](https://www.ogc.org/standards).\n\n## Definition Geodateninfrastruktur\n\nNach dem Geodatenzugangsgesetz (GeoZG) ist eine Geodateninfrastruktur:\n\n> \"Eine Infrastruktur bestehend aus Geodaten, Metadaten und Geodatendiensten, Netzdiensten und -technologien, Vereinbarungen über gemeinsame Nutzung, über Zugang und Verwendung sowie Koordinierungs- und Überwachungsmechanismen, -prozesse und -verfahren mit dem Ziel, Geodaten verschiedener Herkunft interoperabel verfügbar zu machen.\"\n\n## Architektur-Komponenten\n\n### 1. Datenhaltung (Storage Layer)\n\n**PostgreSQL/PostGIS-Datenbank**\n- **Zweck:** Persistente Speicherung aller raumbezogenen Daten\n- **Technologie:** PostgreSQL 15+ mit PostGIS 3.4+ Extension\n- **Datenmodell:** Container-Konzept (einheitliches Schema für Cemetery, Grabflur, Verwaltungsgrenzen)\n- **Koordinatensysteme:** EPSG:25832 (primär), EPSG:3857, EPSG:4326\n\n**GeoTIFF-Rasterdaten**\n- **Zweck:** Georeferenzierte Friedhofspläne als Hintergrundkarten\n- **Format:** Cloud-Optimized GeoTIFF (COG), 8-Bit Grayscale\n- **Auflösung:** ~0.02 m/Pixel (native Scanauflösung)\n- **Pyramidisierung:** 5 Overview-Levels mit Nearest-Neighbor-Resampling\n- **Komprimierung:** DEFLATE, Tiled (512×512px Blocks)\n- **Speicherort:** `/srv/geoserver/data/friedhofsplaene/`\n\n### 2. Geodatenserver (Service Layer)\n\n**GeoServer 2.24+**\n- **Rolle:** OGC-konformer Geodatenserver\n- **Services:**\n  - WMS 1.3.0 (Web Map Service) - Kartenansicht\n  - WFS 2.0.0 (Web Feature Service) - Vektorgeometrien\n  - WFS-T (Transactional) - Live-Editing (in Entwicklung)\n- **Projections:** On-the-fly Reprojection zwischen EPSG:25832, 3857, 4326\n- **Styling:** SLD 1.0 (Styled Layer Descriptor) für Transparenz und Symbolisierung\n- **Endpoint:** `http://192.168.xxx.yyy:8080/geoserver/`\n- **Workspaces:**\n  - `Verwaltungsdaten` (WFS-Features: Friedhöfe, Gräber)\n  - `friedhofsplaene` (WMS-Raster: GeoTIFF-Layer)\n\n**GeoServer-Konfiguration Highlights**\n- **Coverage Stores:** Einzelne GeoTIFFs oder ImageMosaic für Multi-Friedhof-Support\n- **Default Interpolation:** Nearest Neighbor (kritisch für Schwarz-Weiß-Pläne)\n- **SLD-Style:** `friedhofsplan_transparent` wandelt Weiß → Transparent\n\n### 3. Caching & Performance (Middleware Layer)\n\n**MapProxy 1.16+**\n- **Rolle:** Tile-Caching, Request-Aggregation, CORS-Proxy, OSM-Tile-Integration\n- **Cache-Strategie:**\n  - File-basierter Cache unter `/srv/mapproxy/cache_data/`\n  - TMS (Tile Map Service) Directory-Layout\n- **Grids:**\n  - `nrw_grid` (EPSG:25832, 20 Zoom-Levels, √2-Faktor)\n  - `friedhofsplan_grid_utm32` (High-Resolution, 9 Levels, 4.0 → 0.0078 m/px)\n  - `osm_grid` (EPSG:3857, Web Mercator für OSM-Tiles)\n- **Resampling:** Nearest Neighbor (konsistent mit GeoServer)\n- **Endpoint:** `https://ows.data-dna.eu/`\n- **Services:** WMS, WMTS, TMS\n\n#### OSM-Tileserver-Integration\n\n**Status:** Infrastruktur bereitgestellt, noch nicht produktiv\n\nMapProxy dient als Proxy/Cache-Layer für einen lokalen OSM-Tileserver, um langfristig die öffentlichen OSM-Server zu entlasten:\n\n**Aktueller Zustand:**\n- **Upstream:** OSM-Community-Tiles (`tile.openstreetmap.org`) via MapProxy-Durchleitung\n- **Lokaler Tileserver:** Aufgebaut (mod_tile + Mapnik + OSM-Datenbank), aber nicht aktiviert\n- **Endpoint:** `http://192.168.xxx.yyy:8080/tile/%(z)s/%(x)s/%(y)s.png` (intern)\n\n**MapProxy-Konfiguration (aktuell):**\n```\nsources:\n  osm_source:\n    type: tile\n    grid: osm_grid\n    url: http://192.168.xxx.yyy:8080/tile/%(z)s/%(x)s/%(y)s.png  # Lokaler Tileserver (vorbereitet)\n    # url: http://a.tile.openstreetmap.org/%(z)s/%(x)s/%(y)s.png  # Fallback zu OSM\n    http:\n      headers:\n        User-Agent: \"p2d2-MapProxy/1.0\"\n\ngrids:\n  osm_grid:\n    srs: 'EPSG:3857'\n    bbox: [-20037508.34, -20037508.34, 20037508.34, 20037508.34]\n    origin: 'nw'\n```\n\n**Geplante Migration:**\n1. **Phase 1 (aktuell):** OSM-Community-Tiles + lokales MapProxy-Caching\n   - Vorteil: Reduzierte Last auf OSM durch MapProxy-Cache\n   - Nachteil: Initial Requests gehen noch zu OSM\n\n2. **Phase 2 (geplant):** Lokaler Tileserver produktiv\n   - **Stack:** PostgreSQL/PostGIS + osm2pgsql + Mapnik + mod_tile\n   - **Daten:** OSM-Extract für NRW (~5 GB PBF, wöchentliches Update)\n   - **Seeding:** Pre-Caching für Köln/NRW bis Zoom-Level 16\n   - **Vorteil:** Vollständige Unabhängigkeit, keine Last auf OSM-Community\n\n3. **Phase 3 (Zukunft):** Custom OSM-Styles\n   - Angepasste Kartenstylings für Friedhofskontext\n   - Hervorhebung von Points of Interest (Friedhöfe, öffentliche Gebäude)\n\n**Motivation:**\nDie Nutzung öffentlicher OSM-Tileserver ist laut [OSM Tile Usage Policy](https://operations.osmfoundation.org/policies/tiles/) nur für leichte bis moderate Nutzung vorgesehen. Für produktive Anwendungen mit vielen Nutzern empfiehlt die OSM-Community den Betrieb eines eigenen Tileservers oder die Nutzung kommerzieller Anbieter. Mit der lokalen Infrastruktur folgen wir diesem Best Practice und entlasten die Community-Ressourcen nachhaltig.\n\n**Technische Details (Lokaler Tileserver):**\n- **Software:** mod_tile + renderd + Mapnik\n- **Datenbank:** PostgreSQL 15 + PostGIS 3.4 mit OSM-Schema\n- **Speicherbedarf:** ~50 GB für NRW-Datenbank, ~200 GB für Tile-Cache\n- **Update-Strategie:** osmosis für tägliche Diff-Updates\n- **Dokumentation:** Siehe `docs/OSM-TILESERVER.md` (in Vorbereitung)\n\n**MapProxy-Konfiguration für Friedhofspläne:**\n```\nsources:\n  friedhofsplan_rheinkassel_wms:\n    type: wms\n    req:\n      url: http://192.168.xxx.yyy:8080/geoserver/wms\n      layers: friedhofsplaene:rheinkassel_friedhof\n      styles: friedhofsplan_transparent\n    coverage:\n      bbox: [355079.917, 5656115.018, 355310.314, 5656324.347]\n      srs: 'EPSG:25832'\n\ngrids:\n  friedhofsplan_grid_utm32:\n    srs: 'EPSG:25832'\n    bbox:\n    res: [4.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625]\n```\n\n### 4. Frontend (Presentation Layer)\n\n**Astro 4.x + OpenLayers 9.x**\n- **Kartenbibliothek:** OpenLayers 9.1+ (modernes WebGL-Rendering)\n- **Layer-Typen:**\n  - `ImageWMS` für Friedhofspläne (Aspect-Ratio-korrekt)\n  - `TileWMS` für Basemaps (Luftbild, OSM)\n  - `VectorLayer` für WFS-Features (Cemetery-Polygone, Grabfluren)\n- **Koordinatensystem-Handling:**\n  - Dynamischer CRS-Switch (EPSG:3857 ↔ EPSG:25832)\n  - Scale-preserving View-Transitions\n  - UTM-Resolution-Arrays für korrekte Zoom-Levels\n\n**Layer-Hierarchie (Z-Index)**\n```\nZ-Index 15: Labels (geplant)\nZ-Index 10: WFS Features (Cemetery-Polygone, Grabfluren)\nZ-Index  5: Friedhofsplan (ImageWMS)\nZ-Index  1: Luftbild (TileWMS)\nZ-Index  0: Basemap (TileWMS)\n```\n\n**Frontend-Konfiguration**\n```\nconst friedhofsplanLayer = new ImageLayer({\n  source: new ImageWMS({\n    url: 'https://ows.data-dna.eu/service',\n    params: {\n      'LAYERS': 'friedhofsplan_rheinkassel',\n      'FORMAT': 'image/png',\n      'TRANSPARENT': true,\n      'VERSION': '1.1.1',\n    },\n    projection: 'EPSG:25832',\n    serverType: 'geoserver',\n    ratio: 1.0, // Kein Oversampling\n  }),\n  extent: [355079.917, 5656115.018, 355310.314, 5656324.347],\n  zIndex: 5,\n  opacity: 0.7,\n  visible: true,\n});\n```\n\n## Datenfluss\n\n### Kartenansicht (WMS)\n```\nBrowser (OpenLayers)\n  ↓ GetMap-Request (EPSG:25832, BBOX, WIDTH, HEIGHT)\nMapProxy (ows.data-dna.eu)\n  ↓ Cache-Lookup (Tile-Grid)\n  ├─ Cache Hit → Tile zurück\n  └─ Cache Miss → Forward zu GeoServer\n     GeoServer\n       ↓ GeoTIFF-Rendering (Nearest-Neighbor)\n       ↓ SLD-Styling (Transparenz)\n       └─ PNG-Response\n     MapProxy\n       ↓ Tile-Cache (speichern)\n       └─ Tile zurück\nBrowser\n  └─ Anzeige im Canvas\n```\n\n### Feature-Abfrage (WFS)\n```\nBrowser (OpenLayers VectorLayer)\n  ↓ GetFeature-Request (CQL_FILTER, outputFormat=json)\nWFS-Proxy (/api/wfs-proxy)\n  ↓ Basic-Auth-Injection\nGeoServer WFS\n  ↓ SQL-Query zu PostGIS\nPostGIS\n  └─ GeoJSON-Response\nBrowser\n  └─ VectorLayer-Rendering\n```\n\n### Feature-Bearbeitung (WFS-T, geplant)\n```\nBrowser (Draw/Modify-Interactions)\n  ↓ WFS-T Transaction (XML)\nGeoServer WFS-T\n  ↓ INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE\nPostGIS\n  └─ Geometrien persistiert\n```\n\n## Technische Spezifikationen\n\n### Koordinatensysteme\n\n| EPSG-Code | Name | Verwendung | Extent (Deutschland) |\n|-----------|------|------------|---------------------|\n| **25832** | ETRS89 / UTM Zone 32N | Primär (NRW, Köln) | [277000, 5580000, 510000, 5820000] |\n| **3857** | Web Mercator | Basemaps (OSM, Luftbild) | Weltweit |\n| **4326** | WGS 84 | Datenimport/-export | [-180, -90, 180, 90] |\n\n### OGC-Standards\n\n| Standard | Version | Verwendung | Endpoint |\n|----------|---------|------------|----------|\n| WMS | 1.3.0 | Kartenansicht (Raster) | `/geoserver/wms` |\n| WFS | 2.0.0 | Feature-Download | `/geoserver/ows` |\n| WFS-T | 2.0.0 | Feature-Editing | `/geoserver/ows` (geplant) |\n| WMTS | 1.0.0 | Tile-Service | `/service?SERVICE=WMTS` |\n| TMS | 1.0.0 | Tile-Service (REST) | `/tiles/` |\n| SLD | 1.0.0 | Kartenstyling | `/geoserver/styles/` |\n\n### Performance-Optimierungen\n\n1. **GeoTIFF-Optimierung**\n   - Cloud-Optimized Format (COG) für HTTP-Range-Requests\n   - Tiled Struktur (512×512px) für schnellen Partial-Read\n   - DEFLATE-Kompression (~60% Platzeinsparung)\n   - Overviews mit Nearest-Neighbor (keine Qualitätsverluste)\n\n2. **MapProxy-Caching**\n   - Meta-Tiles (4×4) für reduzierten Overhead\n   - Seeding-Strategien für Hotspots\n   - Cache-Invalidierung bei Datenänderungen\n\n3. **Frontend-Optimierung**\n   - ImageWMS statt TileWMS (1 Request statt 10-20)\n   - Custom Resolution-Arrays für präzise Zoom-Levels\n   - Layer-Interaktion-Manager (Long-Press für Opacity)\n\n## Sicherheit & Zugriffskontrolle\n\n### Aktuelle Implementierung\n- **Read-Only WFS:** Basic Authentication (temporäre Credentials)\n- **WMS:** Public Access (keine Auth erforderlich)\n- **MapProxy CORS:** Aktiviert für Browser-Requests\n\n### Geplant (Issue #1)\n- **Anonymous GeoServer Access** für public Geodaten\n- **WFS-T Authentication** via OAuth2/OIDC\n- **Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)** für sensitive Daten\n\n## Skalierbarkeit & Erweiterungen\n\n### Aktueller Zustand\n- **1 Friedhof** (Rheinkassel, 230m×210m)\n- **1 GeoTIFF** (~11.000×10.000 Pixel)\n\n### Zukünftige Erweiterungen\n1. **Multi-Friedhof-Support**\n   - GeoServer ImageMosaic für alle Kölner Friedhöfe\n   - Dynamisches Extent [356000, 5644000, 362000, 5653000]\n   - Automatische Index-Generierung\n\n2. **Weitere Datenschichten**\n   - Grabsteine (Point-Features)\n   - Bepflanzung (Polygon-Features)\n   - Verwaltungsgrenzen (OSM-Import)\n\n3. **WFS-T Live-Editing**\n   - Browser-basierte Geometrie-Erfassung\n   - Conflict-Resolution bei gleichzeitigen Edits\n   - Historisierung & Rollback\n\n## Deployment & Operations\n\n### Infrastruktur\n- **Virtualisierung:** Proxmox VE 8.x\n- **Netzwerk:** OPNsense Firewall\n- **CI/CD:** GitLab CI (Webhook-basiert)\n- **Monitoring:** Uptime-Checks, Log-Aggregation\n\n### URLs\n- **Production:** `https://www.data-dna.eu`\n- **Development:** `https://dev.data-dna.eu`\n- **Operations:** `https://opn.data-dna.eu` (Feature-Editor)\n- **OWS-Endpoint:** `https://ows.data-dna.eu`\n\n### Backup-Strategie\n- **PostGIS:** Tägliches pg_dump (Full + Incrementals)\n- **GeoTIFFs:** Versionskontrolle + Offsite-Backup\n- **GeoServer-Config:** Git-Repository\n\n## Konformität & Standards\n\n- ✅ **GDI-DE-konform:** Interoperabilität mit deutschen GDI-Systemen (angestrebt)\n- ✅ **INSPIRE-kompatibel:** Metadaten nach ISO 19115 (angestrebt)\n- ✅ **OGC-Standards:** WMS, WFS, WMTS, SLD\n- ✅ **Open Source:** Alle Komponenten unter OSI-approved Lizenzen\n\n## Ressourcen\n\n- **GDI-DE Architektur:** [https://www.gdi-de.org/](https://www.gdi-de.org/)\n- **OGC Standards:** [https://www.ogc.org/standards](https://www.ogc.org/standards)\n- **GeoServer Docs:** [https://docs.geoserver.org/](https://docs.geoserver.org/)\n- **MapProxy Docs:** [https://mapproxy.org/docs/](https://mapproxy.org/docs/)\n- **OpenLayers API:** [https://openlayers.org/](https://openlayers.org/)\n- **OSM Tile Usage Policy:** [https://operations.osmfoundation.org/policies/tiles/](https://operations.osmfoundation.org/policies/tiles/)\n- **MapProxy OSM Integration:** [https://wiki.openstreetmap.org/wiki/MapProxy](https://wiki.openstreetmap.org/wiki/MapProxy)\n\n---\n\n**Version:** 1.0.0 (Oktober 2025)\n**Maintainer:** p2d2 Team\n**Lizenz:** MIT (Code), ODbL (Daten)\n\nDiese vollständige Dokumentation kann direkt als `docs/GDI-ARCHITEKTUR.md` im Repository gespeichert werden und enthält alle URLs als Inline-Links statt Fußnoten.\n\n[1](https://www.markdownguide.org/extended-syntax/)\n[2](https://www.markdownguide.org/basic-syntax/)\n[3](https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/writing-on-github/working-with-advanced-formatting/creating-and-highlighting-code-blocks)\n[4](https://www.jetbrains.com/help/hub/markdown-syntax.html)\n[5](https://www.codecademy.com/resources/docs/markdown/code-blocks)\n[6](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/devops/project/wiki/markdown-guidance?view=azure-devops)\n[7](https://confluence.atlassian.com/spaces/BitbucketServer/pages/776639995/Markdown+syntax+guide)\n[8](https://docs.github.com/github/writing-on-github/getting-started-with-writing-and-formatting-on-github/basic-writing-and-formatting-syntax)\n[9](https://docs.stoplight.io/docs/platform/07adf9e4c3f3a-use-markdown-in-documentation)\n[10](https://commonmark.org/help/tutorial/09-code.html)\n"

    "name": "INTEGRATION_POLYGON_SYNC.md",
    "content": "# Polygon Sync Plugin for AstroJS\n\n## Overview\n\nA fully automated AstroJS Integration Plugin that provides real-time polygon synchronization with WFS-T services. The plugin watches markdown files in the kommunen directory and automatically triggers WFS-T synchronization when files are added or modified.\n\n## Features\n\n- ✅ **True AstroJS Integration**: Runs as native Astro integration in both development and production\n- ✅ **Symlink Support**: Full support for symlinked directories with proper file detection\n- ✅ **Background Service**: Continuous operation with graceful shutdown and error recovery\n- ✅ **Debounced Processing**: Prevents multiple rapid syncs with configurable debounce timing\n- ✅ **Production Ready**: Works in both development server and production build environments\n- ✅ **Extensive Logging**: Debug logging for development and troubleshooting\n\n## Installation\n\nThe plugin is already integrated into the project. No additional installation required.\n\n## Configuration\n\nThe plugin is configured in `astro.config.mjs`:\n\n```javascript\nimport { polygonSyncPlugin } from './src/integrations/polygon-sync-plugin';\n\nexport default defineConfig({\n  integrations: [\n    polygonSyncPlugin({\n      watchDir: 'src/content/kommunen',    // Directory to watch\n      autoSync: true,                     // Enable automatic synchronization\n      followSymlinks: true,               // Follow symlinks (important for server environments)\n      debounceMs: 2000,                   // Debounce time in milliseconds\n      debug: process.env.DEBUG === 'true' // Enable debug logging\n    })\n  ]\n});\n```\n\n## Usage\n\n### Automatic Operation\n\nThe plugin starts automatically when the Astro development server starts (`astro dev`) and runs in the background. It will:\n\n1. Watch for new markdown files in `src/content/kommunen/*.md`\n2. Watch for changes to existing markdown files\n3. Extract the slug from the filename (e.g., `berlin.md` → `berlin`)\n4. Trigger `syncKommunePolygons(slug)` with proper debouncing\n5. Log results and handle errors automatically\n\n### Manual Triggering\n\nYou can manually trigger synchronization using the watcher service:\n\n```typescript\nimport { PolygonWatcherService } from './src/services/polygon-watcher-service';\n\nconst watcher = new PolygonWatcherService({\n  watchDir: 'src/content/kommunen',\n  followSymlinks: true,\n  debounceMs: 2000,\n  debug: true\n});\n\n// Trigger sync for specific kommune\nawait watcher.triggerManualSync('berlin');\n```\n\n### Environment Variables\n\n- `DEBUG=true`: Enable detailed debug logging\n- `NODE_ENV=development`: Development mode with enhanced logging\n\n## File Structure\n\n```\nsrc/\n├── integrations/\n│   └── polygon-sync-plugin.ts     # Main Astro integration\n├── services/\n│   └── polygon-watcher-service.ts # Background watcher service\n├── utils/\n│   ├── logger.ts                  # Logging utility\n│   └── polygon-wfst-sync.ts       # Existing sync functionality\n```\n\n## Technical Details\n\n### Chokidar Configuration\n\nThe plugin uses `chokidar` with optimal configuration for server environments:\n\n```typescript\n{\n  persistent: true,\n  followSymlinks: true,           // Critical for symlink support\n  usePolling: process.platform === 'linux', // Required for server environments\n  awaitWriteFinish: {             // Ensures file stability\n    stabilityThreshold: 500,\n    pollInterval: 100\n  },\n  ignoreInitial: true,\n  depth: 1,\n  atomic: true\n}\n```\n\n### Error Handling\n\n- Automatic restart on file watching errors\n- Retry logic with exponential backoff\n- Comprehensive error logging\n- Graceful shutdown handling\n\n### Performance Considerations\n\n- Minimal overhead in development mode\n- Debouncing prevents excessive WFS-T calls\n- Polling only enabled on Linux servers\n- Memory-efficient file watching\n\n## Development\n\n### Testing\n\nRun the test script to verify functionality:\n\n```bash\nnpm run test-polygon-sync\n```\n\n### Debugging\n\nEnable debug mode to see detailed logs:\n\n```bash\nDEBUG=true astro dev\n```\n\n### Adding New Features\n\n1. Extend the `PolygonSyncPluginOptions` interface\n2. Update the watcher service implementation\n3. Add corresponding configuration in `astro.config.mjs`\n4. Update this documentation\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### Common Issues\n\n1. **Files not being detected**: Check symlink configuration and file permissions\n2. **Sync not triggering**: Verify the markdown file is in the correct directory\n3. **Performance issues**: Adjust debounce timing or disable polling if not on Linux\n\n### Log Analysis\n\nCheck the Astro server logs for:\n- File detection events\n- Sync initiation and completion\n- Error messages and stack traces\n\n## Dependencies\n\n- `chokidar`: File watching with symlink support\n- Built-in Astro integration system\n- Existing `polygon-wfst-sync.ts` functionality\n\n## License\n\nPart of the P2D2 project. See main project documentation for licensing details."

    "name": "src/utils/map-state.ts",
    "content": "/**\n * Map State Management\n * Centralized state management for map-related data\n */\n\nexport interface MapState {\n  activeCRS: string;\n  localCRS: string | undefined;\n  selectedCategory: string | null;\n  selectedKommune: any | null;\n  isInitialized: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface MapConfig {\n  defaultCRS: string;\n  wmsUrl: string;\n  wmsLayer: string;\n}\n\nclass MapStateManager {\n  private state: MapState;\n  private config: MapConfig;\n  private listeners: Set<(state: MapState) => void> = new Set();\n\n  constructor(config: MapConfig) {\n    this.config = config;\n    this.state = {\n      activeCRS: config.defaultCRS,\n      localCRS: undefined,\n      selectedCategory: null,\n      selectedKommune: null,\n      isInitialized: false,\n    };\n  }\n\n  getState(): Readonly<MapState> {\n    return { ...this.state };\n  }\n\n  getConfig(): Readonly<MapConfig> {\n    return { ...this.config };\n  }\n\n  updateState(updates: Partial<MapState>): void {\n    const oldState = { ...this.state };\n    this.state = { ...this.state, ...updates };\n\n    // Notify listeners of state changes\n    this.listeners.forEach((listener) => {\n      try {\n        listener(this.getState());\n      } catch (error) {\n        console.warn(\"[map-state] Listener error:\", error);\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  subscribe(listener: (state: MapState) => void): () => void {\n    this.listeners.add(listener);\n\n    // Return unsubscribe function\n    return () => {\n      this.listeners.delete(listener);\n    };\n  }\n\n  setActiveCRS(crs: string): void {\n    this.updateState({ activeCRS: crs });\n\n    // Persist to localStorage\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(\"selectedCRS\", crs);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\"[map-state] Could not persist CRS:\", error);\n    }\n  }\n\n  setLocalCRS(crs: string | undefined): void {\n    this.updateState({ localCRS: crs });\n  }\n\n  setSelectedCategory(category: string | null): void {\n    this.updateState({ selectedCategory: category });\n  }\n\n  setInitialized(initialized: boolean): void {\n    this.updateState({ isInitialized: initialized });\n  }\n\n  setSelectedKommune(kommune: any | null): void {\n    this.updateState({ selectedKommune: kommune });\n  }\n\n  getSelectedKommune(): any | null {\n    return this.state.selectedKommune;\n  }\n\n  getSelectedCategory(): string | null {\n    return this.state.selectedCategory;\n  }\n\n  // Restore state from localStorage\n  restoreFromStorage(): void {\n    try {\n      const savedCRS = localStorage.getItem(\"selectedCRS\");\n      const savedCategory = localStorage.getItem(\"selectedCategory\");\n      const savedKommune = localStorage.getItem(\"selectedMunicipalityDetail\");\n\n      if (savedCRS) {\n        this.updateState({ activeCRS: savedCRS });\n      }\n\n      if (savedCategory) {\n        this.updateState({ selectedCategory: savedCategory });\n      }\n\n      if (savedKommune) {\n        try {\n          const kommuneData = JSON.parse(savedKommune);\n          this.updateState({ selectedKommune: kommuneData });\n        } catch (error) {\n          console.warn(\"[map-state] Could not parse saved kommune:\", error);\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\"[map-state] Could not restore from storage:\", error);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// Export singleton instance\nexport const mapState = new MapStateManager({\n  defaultCRS: \"EPSG:3857\",\n  wmsUrl: \"https://ows.data-dna.eu/\",\n  wmsLayer: \"p2d2_cemeteries_cologne\",\n});\n"
