    "name": "src/utils/feature-popup-handler.ts",
    "content": "/**\n * Feature Popup Handler for p2d2 OpenLayers Map\n * Handles cemetery feature clicks, popup display, and WFS data integration\n */\n\nimport { Map as OLMap } from \"ol\";\nimport { Feature } from \"ol\";\nimport { Geometry } from \"ol/geom\";\nimport { transformExtent } from \"ol/proj\";\nimport VectorLayer from \"ol/layer/Vector\";\nimport VectorSource from \"ol/source/Vector\";\nimport Overlay from \"ol/Overlay\";\nimport { wfsAuthClient } from \"./wfs-auth\";\nimport { mapState } from \"./map-state\";\n\n// Type definitions for feature properties\ninterface CemeteryFeatureProperties {\n  name: string;\n  container_type: string;\n  wp_name: string;\n  osm_admin_level: number;\n  [key: string]: any;\n}\n\ninterface GrabflurFeatureProperties {\n  name: string;\n  container_type: string;\n  wp_name: string;\n  osm_admin_level: number;\n  [key: string]: any;\n}\n\ninterface PopupContent {\n  cemetery: CemeteryFeatureProperties;\n  grabflurFeatures: GrabflurFeatureProperties[];\n  error?: string;\n}\n\nexport class FeaturePopupHandler {\n  private map: OLMap;\n  private modalElement: HTMLDialogElement | null = null;\n  private isInitialized: boolean = false;\n  private backdropClickHandler: ((event: MouseEvent) => void) | null = null;\n\n  constructor(map: OLMap) {\n    this.map = map;\n    this.initializeModal();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Initialize the modal dialog system\n   */\n  private initializeModal(): void {\n    // Create modal dialog element\n    this.modalElement = document.createElement(\"dialog\");\n    this.modalElement.className = \"feature-popup-modal\";\n    this.modalElement.style.cssText = `\n      padding: 0;\n      box-sizing: border-box;\n      border: none;\n      border-radius: 0.75rem;\n      box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);\n      min-width: 280px;\n      max-width: min(90vw, 360px);\n      width: auto;\n      max-height: 80vh;\n      overflow-y: auto;\n      overflow-x: hidden;\n      text-align: left;\n      position: fixed;\n      top: 50%;\n      left: 50%;\n      transform: translate(-50%, -50%);\n      word-wrap: break-word;\n      word-break: break-word;\n      z-index: 10000;\n    `;\n\n    // Add modal to document body\n    document.body.appendChild(this.modalElement);\n    this.isInitialized = true;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Initialize click event handler for cemetery features\n   */\n  public initializeClickHandler(): void {\n    if (!this.isInitialized) {\n      console.warn(\"[FeaturePopup] Handler not initialized\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    this.map.on(\"click\", async (event) => {\n      try {\n        const feature = this.findCemeteryFeatureAtPixel(event.pixel);\n\n        if (feature) {\n          await this.handleCemeteryClick(feature, event.coordinate);\n        } else {\n          this.closePopup();\n        }\n      } catch (error) {\n        console.error(\"[FeaturePopup] Click handler error:\", error);\n        this.closePopup();\n      }\n    });\n\n    console.log(\"[FeaturePopup] Click handler initialized\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Find cemetery feature at pixel position\n   */\n  private findCemeteryFeatureAtPixel(\n    pixel: number[],\n  ): Feature<Geometry> | null {\n    let foundFeature: Feature<Geometry> | null = null;\n\n    this.map.forEachFeatureAtPixel(pixel, (feature) => {\n      if (this.isCemeteryFeature(feature)) {\n        foundFeature = feature as Feature<Geometry>;\n        return feature; // Stop iteration\n      }\n      return undefined; // Continue iteration\n    });\n\n    return foundFeature;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check if feature is a cemetery\n   */\n  private isCemeteryFeature(feature: any): boolean {\n    if (!feature || typeof feature.getProperties !== \"function\") {\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    const properties = feature.getProperties();\n    const activeContainerType = this.getActiveContainerType();\n\n    return properties?.container_type === activeContainerType;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get container type for currently selected category\n   * Returns 'cemetery' as fallback if no category selected\n   */\n  private getActiveContainerType(): string {\n    const categorySlug = mapState.getSelectedCategory();\n\n    if (!categorySlug) {\n      console.warn(\n        '[FeaturePopup] No category selected, using fallback \"cemetery\"',\n      );\n      return \"cemetery\";\n    }\n\n    // Try to get from embedded category data\n    try {\n      const categoryDataElement = document.getElementById(\"category-data\");\n      if (categoryDataElement) {\n        const categoryMapStr =\n          categoryDataElement.getAttribute(\"data-category-map\");\n        if (categoryMapStr) {\n          const categoryMap = JSON.parse(categoryMapStr);\n          if (categoryMap[categorySlug]?.containerType) {\n            return categoryMap[categorySlug].containerType;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\n        \"[FeaturePopup] Could not load category data, using fallback\",\n      );\n    }\n\n    // Hardcoded fallback mapping\n    const fallbackMapping: Record<string, string> = {\n      cemeteries: \"cemetery\",\n      administrative: \"administrative\",\n    };\n\n    return fallbackMapping[categorySlug] || \"cemetery\";\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle cemetery feature click\n   */\n  private async handleCemeteryClick(\n    feature: Feature<Geometry>,\n    coordinate: number[],\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const properties = feature.getProperties() as CemeteryFeatureProperties;\n    const geometry = feature.getGeometry();\n\n    if (!geometry) {\n      console.warn(\"[FeaturePopup] Cemetery feature has no geometry\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      // Check if sub-features exist before opening editor\n      const grabflurFeatures = await this.loadGrabflurData(properties);\n\n      if (grabflurFeatures.length > 0) {\n        // Open feature editor in new window\n        this.openFeatureEditor(properties, geometry);\n      } else {\n        // Show simple info popup\n        this.showInfoPopup(coordinate, properties);\n      }\n\n      // Zoom to feature\n      this.zoomToFeature(geometry);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"[FeaturePopup] Failed to handle cemetery click:\", error);\n      this.showInfoPopup(coordinate, properties);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Load grabflur data for cemetery\n   */\n  private async loadGrabflurData(\n    cemeteryProps: CemeteryFeatureProperties,\n  ): Promise<GrabflurFeatureProperties[]> {\n    // Build CQL filter for grabflur features\n    // Das % für SQL LIKE wird im URL-Encoding automatisch korrekt behandelt\n    const namePattern = `${cemeteryProps.name}-%`;\n\n    // Get container type dynamically from selected category\n    const containerType = this.getActiveContainerType();\n\n    // Use correct CQL filter syntax with DYNAMIC container_type\n    const cqlFilter = `osm_admin_level=10 AND wp_name='${cemeteryProps.wp_name}' AND container_type='${containerType}' AND (name LIKE '${namePattern}')`;\n\n    console.log(\n      `[FeaturePopup] Loading grabflur data with container_type=\"${containerType}\"`,\n    );\n    console.log(\"[FeaturePopup] CQL Filter:\", cqlFilter);\n\n    try {\n      // Use the corrected buildAuthorizedWFSURL method that now handles encoding correctly\n      const url = wfsAuthClient.buildAuthorizedWFSURL(\"p2d2_containers\", {\n        CQL_FILTER: cqlFilter,\n      });\n\n      console.log(\"[FeaturePopup] Using corrected WFS URL:\", url);\n\n      const response = await wfsAuthClient.fetchWithAuth(url);\n\n      if (!response.ok) {\n        throw new Error(`WFS request failed: ${response.status}`);\n      }\n\n      const geoJson = await response.json();\n\n      if (!geoJson.features || !Array.isArray(geoJson.features)) {\n        console.warn(\"[FeaturePopup] No features in response\");\n        return [];\n      }\n\n      // Extract properties from features\n      const grabflurFeatures: GrabflurFeatureProperties[] =\n        geoJson.features.map((feature: any) => {\n          return {\n            name: feature.properties?.name || \"Unbenannt\",\n            container_type: feature.properties?.container_type || \"cemetery\",\n            wp_name: feature.properties?.wp_name || cemeteryProps.wp_name,\n            osm_admin_level: feature.properties?.osm_admin_level || 10,\n            ...feature.properties,\n          };\n        });\n\n      console.log(\n        `[FeaturePopup] Loaded ${grabflurFeatures.length} grabflur features`,\n      );\n      return grabflurFeatures;\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"[FeaturePopup] Failed to load grabflur data:\", error);\n      throw error;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Show popup with cemetery and grabflur data as modal dialog\n   */\n  private showPopup(coordinate: number[], content: PopupContent): void {\n    if (!this.modalElement) {\n      console.error(\"[FeaturePopup] Modal element not initialized\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Generate popup HTML with modal structure\n    const modalHTML = `${this.generatePopupHTML(content)}`;\n\n    // Set modal content\n    this.modalElement.innerHTML = modalHTML;\n\n    // Show modal     "name": "src/utils/view-history-manager.ts",
    "content": "import type { View } from \"ol\";\n\ninterface ViewState {\n  center: number[];\n  zoom: number;\n  timestamp: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Manages view history for back/forward navigation in Feature Editor\n * Use Case: Overview → Zoom to Grabflur → Back → Zoom to Grab → Back to Grabflur\n */\nexport class ViewHistoryManager {\n  private history: ViewState[] = [];\n  private currentIndex: number = -1;\n  private view: View;\n  private maxHistorySize: number = 20;\n  private lastPushTime: number = 0;\n  private readonly PUSH_DEBOUNCE = 500; // ms\n\n  constructor(view: View, maxHistorySize: number = 20) {\n    this.view = view;\n    this.maxHistorySize = maxHistorySize;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Save current view state to history\n   * Called after significant zoom/pan actions\n   */\n  pushState(): void {\n    // Debounce: Ignore pushes within 500ms of last push\n    const now = Date.now();\n    if (now - this.lastPushTime < this.PUSH_DEBOUNCE) {\n      console.log(\"ViewHistory: Push debounced\");\n      return;\n    }\n    this.lastPushTime = now;\n\n    const center = this.view.getCenter();\n    const zoom = this.view.getZoom();\n\n    if (!center || zoom === undefined) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Remove any future states if we're not at the end\n    if (this.currentIndex < this.history.length - 1) {\n      this.history = this.history.slice(0, this.currentIndex + 1);\n    }\n\n    // Add new state\n    this.history.push({\n      center: [...center],\n      zoom,\n      timestamp: Date.now(),\n    });\n\n    // Limit history size\n    if (this.history.length > this.maxHistorySize) {\n      this.history.shift();\n    } else {\n      this.currentIndex++;\n    }\n\n    console.log(\"ViewHistory: Pushed state\", {\n      index: this.currentIndex,\n      total: this.history.length,\n      zoom,\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Navigate back in history\n   */\n  back(): boolean {\n    if (!this.canGoBack()) {\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    this.currentIndex--;\n    const state = this.history[this.currentIndex];\n\n    this.view.animate({\n      center: state.center,\n      zoom: state.zoom,\n      duration: 300,\n    });\n\n    console.log(\"ViewHistory: Navigated back to index\", this.currentIndex);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Navigate forward in history\n   */\n  forward(): boolean {\n    if (!this.canGoForward()) {\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    this.currentIndex++;\n    const state = this.history[this.currentIndex];\n\n    this.view.animate({\n      center: state.center,\n      zoom: state.zoom,\n      duration: 300,\n    });\n\n    console.log(\"ViewHistory: Navigated forward to index\", this.currentIndex);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check if back navigation is possible\n   */\n  canGoBack(): boolean {\n    return this.currentIndex > 0;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check if forward navigation is possible\n   */\n  canGoForward(): boolean {\n    return this.currentIndex < this.history.length - 1;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get current history state\n   */\n  getState() {\n    return {\n      currentIndex: this.currentIndex,\n      historyLength: this.history.length,\n      canGoBack: this.canGoBack(),\n      canGoForward: this.canGoForward(),\n    };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Clear all history\n   */\n  clear(): void {\n    this.history = [];\n    this.currentIndex = -1;\n  }\n}\n"

    "name": "src/utils/events.ts",
    "content": "/**\n * Robust Event Handling with Retry Mechanism\n * Provides reliable event dispatching for p2d2 kommune focus events\n */\n\n// Event queue for retry mechanism\ninterface QueuedEvent {\n  eventName: string;\n  detail: any;\n  timestamp: number;\n  retryCount: number;\n  maxRetries: number;\n}\n\n// Global event queue and processing state\nconst eventQueue: QueuedEvent[] = [];\nlet isProcessingQueue = false;\nconst MAX_RETRIES = 3;\nconst RETRY_DELAY = 250; // ms\nconst QUEUE_PROCESS_INTERVAL = 100; // ms\n\n// Throttle tracking\nconst lastDispatchTimes = new Map<string, number>();\nconst THROTTLE_MS = 200; // Reduced for better responsiveness\n\n// Event type definitions\nexport const EVENT_KOMMUNEN_FOCUS = \"kommunen:focus\";\n\n// Interface für Kommunen Focus Event Detail\ninterface KommunenFocusDetail {\n  center?: [number, number];\n  extent?: [number, number, number, number];\n  zoom?: number;\n  projection?: string;\n  extra?: any;\n  slug?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Enhanced throttle implementation with queue integration\n */\nfunction throttle<T extends (...args: any[]) => void>(\n  func: T,\n  delay: number,\n): (...args: Parameters<T>) => void {\n  let timeoutId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;\n  let lastExecTime = 0;\n\n  return (...args: Parameters<T>) => {\n    const currentTime = Date.now();\n\n    if (currentTime - lastExecTime < delay) {\n      if (timeoutId) {\n        clearTimeout(timeoutId);\n      }\n      timeoutId = setTimeout(\n        () => {\n          lastExecTime = currentTime;\n          func(...args);\n        },\n        delay - (currentTime - lastExecTime),\n      );\n    } else {\n      lastExecTime = currentTime;\n      func(...args);\n    }\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if event system is ready\n */\nfunction isEventSystemReady(): boolean {\n  return (\n    typeof window !== \"undefined\" &&\n    window.dispatchEvent !== undefined &&\n    document !== undefined &&\n    document.readyState !== \"loading\"\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Process event queue with retry mechanism\n */\nfunction processEventQueue(): void {\n  if (isProcessingQueue || eventQueue.length === 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Prevent recursive calls\n  if ((window as any).__p2d2ProcessingQueue) return;\n  (window as any).__p2d2ProcessingQueue = true;\n\n  isProcessingQueue = true;\n\n  while (eventQueue.length > 0) {\n    const queuedEvent = eventQueue.shift();\n    if (!queuedEvent) break;\n\n    try {\n      if (isEventSystemReady()) {\n        // Dispatch the event\n        window.dispatchEvent(\n          new CustomEvent(queuedEvent.eventName, {\n            detail: queuedEvent.detail,\n          }),\n        );\n        console.log(\n          `[events] successfully dispatched ${queuedEvent.eventName} after ${queuedEvent.retryCount} retries`,\n        );\n      } else {\n        // Event system not ready, requeue with retry\n        if (queuedEvent.retryCount < queuedEvent.maxRetries) {\n          queuedEvent.retryCount++;\n          eventQueue.unshift(queuedEvent);\n          console.log(\n            `[events] requeuing ${queuedEvent.eventName}, retry ${queuedEvent.retryCount}/${queuedEvent.maxRetries}`,\n          );\n        } else {\n          console.warn(\n            `[events] max retries exceeded for ${queuedEvent.eventName}`,\n          );\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\n        `[events] error dispatching ${queuedEvent.eventName}:`,\n        error,\n      );\n\n      // Requeue on error if retries remain\n      if (queuedEvent.retryCount < queuedEvent.maxRetries) {\n        queuedEvent.retryCount++;\n        eventQueue.unshift(queuedEvent);\n        console.log(\n          `[events] requeuing after error, retry ${queuedEvent.retryCount}/${queuedEvent.maxRetries}`,\n        );\n      } else {\n        console.warn(\n          `[events] max retries exceeded after error for ${queuedEvent.eventName}`,\n        );\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  isProcessingQueue = false;\n  (window as any).__p2d2ProcessingQueue = false;\n\n  // Continue processing if queue is not empty\n  if (eventQueue.length > 0) {\n    setTimeout(processEventQueue, QUEUE_PROCESS_INTERVAL);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Queue event for reliable dispatch with retry mechanism\n */\nfunction queueEvent(\n  eventName: string,\n  detail: any = {},\n  maxRetries: number = MAX_RETRIES,\n): void {\n  const queuedEvent: QueuedEvent = {\n    eventName,\n    detail,\n    timestamp: Date.now(),\n    retryCount: 0,\n    maxRetries,\n  };\n\n  eventQueue.push(queuedEvent);\n\n  // Start processing if not already running\n  if (!isProcessingQueue) {\n    setTimeout(processEventQueue, QUEUE_PROCESS_INTERVAL);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Dispatch a throttled event with retry mechanism\n */\nexport function dispatchThrottledEvent(\n  eventName: string,\n  detail: any = {},\n  throttleMs: number = THROTTLE_MS,\n): void {\n  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") {\n    console.warn(`[events] cannot dispatch ${eventName} - window undefined`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const lastDispatch = lastDispatchTimes.get(eventName) || 0;\n  const currentTime = Date.now();\n\n  if (currentTime - lastDispatch < throttleMs) {\n    console.log(`[events] throttling ${eventName} - too frequent`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  lastDispatchTimes.set(eventName, currentTime);\n  queueEvent(eventName, detail);\n}\n\n/**\n * Dispatch kommunen focus event with robust retry mechanism\n */\nexport function dispatchKommunenFocus(detail: KommunenFocusDetail): void {\n  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") {\n    console.warn(\"[events] cannot dispatch kommunen focus - window undefined\");\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Validate data before dispatch\n  const hasValidCenter = isValidWgs84Coordinate(detail.center);\n  const hasValidExtent = isValidWgs84Extent(detail.extent);\n\n  if (!hasValidCenter && !hasValidExtent) {\n    console.warn(\n      \"[events] skipping kommunen focus - no valid center or extent data\",\n      detail,\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Use robust queuing system instead of simple timeout\n  queueEvent(\"kommunen:focus\", detail, MAX_RETRIES);\n  console.log(\"[events] queued kommunen focus for reliable dispatch\");\n}\n\nfunction isValidWgs84Coordinate(coord: any): coord is [number, number] {\n  return (\n    Array.isArray(coord) &&\n    coord.length === 2 &&\n    coord.every(Number.isFinite) &&\n    coord[0] >= -180 &&\n    coord[0] <= 180 &&\n    coord[1] >= -90 &&\n    coord[1] <= 90\n  );\n}\n\nfunction isValidWgs84Extent(\n  extent: any,\n): extent is [number, number, number, number] {\n  return (\n    Array.isArray(extent) &&\n    extent.length === 4 &&\n    extent.every(Number.isFinite) &&\n    extent[0] >= -180 &&\n    extent[2] <= 180 &&\n    extent[1] >= -90 &&\n    extent[3] <= 90\n  );\n}\n\n// Make dispatchKommunenFocus globally available for event delegation\nif (typeof window !== \"undefined\") {\n  (window as any).dispatchKommunenFocus = dispatchKommunenFocus;\n  (window as any).dispatchThrottledEvent = dispatchThrottledEvent;\n\n  // Initialize event system on document ready\n  if (document.readyState === \"loading\") {\n    document.addEventListener(\"DOMContentLoaded\", () => {\n      console.log(\"[events] DOM loaded, starting event queue processing\");\n      processEventQueue();\n    });\n  } else {\n    console.log(\"[events] DOM already ready, starting event queue processing\");\n    processEventQueue();\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Add event listener with HMR guard\n */\nexport function addEventListener(\n  eventName: string,\n  handler: (event: any) => void,\n  options?: AddEventListenerOptions,\n): void {\n  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") return;\n\n  // Create unique handler key for HMR deduplication\n  const handlerKey = `__${eventName}_handler_${Date.now()}__`;\n\n  // Check if handler already exists and remove it\n  const existingHandler = (window as any)[handlerKey];\n  if (existingHandler) {\n    window.removeEventListener(eventName, existingHandler, options);\n  }\n\n  // Store handler reference and add listener\n  (window as any)[handlerKey] = handler;\n  window.addEventListener(eventName, handler, options);\n}\n\n// Local storage keys\nconst STORAGE_KEYS = {\n  SELECTED_CRS: \"p2d2_selected_crs\",\n  SELECTED_KOMMUNE: \"p2d2_selected_kommune\",\n};\n\n/**\n * Get selected CRS from local storage\n */\nexport function getSelectedCRS(): string | null {\n  if (typeof window result.success = false;\n      }\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    result.errors.push((error as Error).message);\n    result.success = false;\n  }\n\n  return result;\n}\n\n// Python-Script </wfs:Insert>\n</wfs:Transaction>`;\n}\n"

    "content": "import { getCollection } from \"astro:content\";\n\n// Cache for category mappings to avoid repeated content collection calls\nlet categoryMappingsCache: Map<string, string> | null = null;\nlet cacheTimestamp: number = 0;\nconst CACHE_DURATION_MS = 30000; // 30 seconds cache\n\n/**\n * Interface for category data with container type mapping\n */\nexport interface KategorieData {\n  slug: string;\n  title: string;\n  icon: string;\n  order: number;\n  description: string;\n  containerType?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get all categories with their container mappings from content collections\n */\nexport async function getAllKategorien(): Promise<KategorieData[]> {\n  try {\n    const kategorienCollection = await getCollection(\"kategorien\");\n\n    return kategorienCollection\n      .map(kategorie => ({\n        slug: kategorie.slug,\n        title: kategorie.data.title,\n        icon: kategorie.data.icon,\n        order: kategorie.data.order,\n        description: kategorie.data.description,\n        containerType: kategorie.data.containerType\n      }))\n      .sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order);\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Error loading kategorien:\", error);\n    return [];\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Get dynamic category to container type mapping\n * Uses caching for better performance\n */\nexport async function getKategorieContainerMapping(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {\n  // Return cached result if available and not expired\n  if (categoryMappingsCache && Date.now() - cacheTimestamp < CACHE_DURATION_MS) {\n    return Object.fromEntries(categoryMappingsCache);\n  }\n\n  const kategorien = await getAllKategorien();\n  const mapping: Record<string, string> = {};\n\n  for (const kategorie of kategorien) {\n    if (kategorie.containerType) {\n      mapping[kategorie.slug] = kategorie.containerType;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Update cache\n  categoryMappingsCache = new Map(Object.entries(mapping));\n  cacheTimestamp = Date.now();\n\n  return mapping;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get container type for a specific category slug\n * Returns null if category not found or no container type defined\n */\nexport async function getContainerTypeForCategory(slug: string): Promise<string | null> {\n  const mapping = await getKategorieContainerMapping();\n  return mapping[slug] || null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a category exists and has a container type mapping\n */\nexport async function hasContainerType(slug: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  const mapping = await getKategorieContainerMapping();\n  return slug in mapping;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get all category slugs that have container type mappings\n */\nexport async function getMappedCategorySlugs(): Promise<string[]> {\n  const mapping = await getKategorieContainerMapping();\n  return Object.keys(mapping);\n}\n\n/**\n * Clear the category mappings cache\n * Useful for development or when content changes\n */\nexport function clearKategorieCache(): void {\n  categoryMappingsCache = null;\n  cacheTimestamp = 0;\n}\n"

    "content": "/**\n * Map Navigation Utilities for Feature Editor\n * Provides functions for zoom, reset, and navigation controls\n * Compatible with existing ViewHistoryManager implementation\n */\n\nimport type { Map as OLMap } from \"ol\";\nimport { ViewHistoryManager } from \"./view-history-manager\";\nimport { MAP_CONFIG } from \"../config/map-config\";\n\n// Global variable to store ViewHistoryManager instances per map\nconst viewHistoryManagers = new WeakMap<OLMap, ViewHistoryManager>();\n\n/**\n * Zoom in on the map\n */\nexport function zoomIn(map: OLMap): void {\n  const view = map.getView();\n  const currentZoom = view.getZoom();\n  if (currentZoom !== undefined) {\n    view.setZoom(currentZoom + 1);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Zoom out on the map\n */\nexport function zoomOut(map: OLMap): void {\n  const view = map.getView();\n  const currentZoom = view.getZoom();\n  if (currentZoom !== undefined && currentZoom > 0) {\n    view.setZoom(currentZoom - 1);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Reset view to initial configuration\n */\nexport function resetView(map: OLMap): void {\n  const view = map.getView();\n  view.setCenter(MAP_CONFIG.INITIAL_CENTER);\n  view.setZoom(MAP_CONFIG.INITIAL_ZOOM);\n}\n\n/**\n * Navigate back in view history\n */\nexport function goBack(map: OLMap): void {\n  const viewHistory = getViewHistoryManager(map);\n  if (viewHistory?.back()) {\n    // Mark as programmatic to prevent double-push\n    (map as any).isProgrammaticZoom = true;\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      (map as any).isProgrammaticZoom = false;\n    }, 400);\n    updateNavButtons(map);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Navigate forward in view history\n */\nexport function goForward(map: OLMap): void {\n  const viewHistory = getViewHistoryManager(map);\n  if (viewHistory?.forward()) {\n    // Mark as programmatic to prevent double-push\n    (map as any).isProgrammaticZoom = true;\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      (map as any).isProgrammaticZoom = false;\n    }, 400);\n    updateNavButtons(map);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Get the ViewHistoryManager instance for a map\n */\nexport function getViewHistoryManager(map: OLMap): ViewHistoryManager | null {\n  return viewHistoryManagers.get(map) || null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Update navigation button states based on history\n */\nfunction updateNavButtons(map: OLMap): void {\n  const backBtn = document.getElementById(\"nav-back\") as HTMLButtonElement;\n  const fwdBtn = document.getElementById(\"nav-forward\") as HTMLButtonElement;\n\n  if (!backBtn || !fwdBtn) return;\n\n  const viewHistory = getViewHistoryManager(map);\n  if (!viewHistory) return;\n\n  const state = viewHistory.getState();\n  backBtn.disabled = !state.canGoBack;\n  fwdBtn.disabled = !state.canGoForward;\n}\n\n/**\n * Initialize navigation controls for a map\n * This should be called after the map is created\n */\nexport function initNavigationControls(map: OLMap): void {\n  // Create and store ViewHistoryManager for this map\n  const viewHistory = new ViewHistoryManager(map.getView());\n  viewHistoryManagers.set(map, viewHistory);\n\n  // Flag to prevent double-push during programmatic zooms\n  (map as any).isProgrammaticZoom = false;\n\n  // Save initial view as first history state\n  viewHistory.pushState();\n\n  // Update button states initially\n  updateNavButtons(map);\n\n  // Listen for view changes to update history and buttons\n  let changeTimeout: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;\n\n  const handleViewChange = () => {\n    // Ignore if this is a programmatic zoom\n    if ((map as any).isProgrammaticZoom) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Debounce view changes to avoid too many history entries\n    if (changeTimeout) {\n      clearTimeout(changeTimeout);\n    }\n\n    changeTimeout = setTimeout(() => {\n      viewHistory.pushState();\n      updateNavButtons(map);\n    }, 100);\n  };\n\n  map.getView().on(\"change:center\", handleViewChange);\n  map.getView().on(\"change:resolution\", handleViewChange);\n\n  // Cleanup function for when map is destroyed\n  (map as any).__navigationCleanup = () => {\n    if (changeTimeout) {\n      clearTimeout(changeTimeout);\n    }\n    map.getView().un(\"change:center\", handleViewChange);\n    map.getView().un(\"change:resolution\", handleViewChange);\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Clean up navigation controls when map is destroyed\n */\nexport function cleanupNavigationControls(map: OLMap): void {\n  const cleanup = (map as any).__navigationCleanup;\n  if (cleanup) {\n    cleanup();\n  }\n  viewHistoryManagers.delete(map);\n}\n"

\"ol/layer/Vector\";\nimport VectorSource from \"ol/source/Vector\";\nimport { Style, Stroke, Fill } from \"ol/style\";\nimport GeoJSON from \"ol/format/GeoJSON\";\nimport { wfsAuthClient } from \"./wfs-auth\";\n\n// Type definitions\ninterface WFSLayerConfig {\n  wpName: string;\n  containerType: string;\n  osmAdminLevel: number;\n}\n\ninterface KommuneData {\n  slug: string;\n  wp_name: string;\n  osmAdminLevels: number[];\n}\n\nexport class WFSLayerManager {\n  private map: OLMap;\n  private activeLayer: VectorLayer<VectorSource> | null = null;\n  private currentState: {\n    kommune: KommuneData | null;\n    categorySlug: string | null;\n  } = { kommune: null, categorySlug: null };\n  private layerCache = new Map<string, VectorLayer<VectorSource>>();\n\n  constructor(map: OLMap) {\n    this.map = map;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Toggle WFS layer for kommune/kategorie combination\n   */\n  async toggleLayer(kommune: KommuneData, categorySlug: string): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      const isSameSelection = this.isSameSelection(kommune, categorySlug);\n\n      if (isSameSelection && this.activeLayer) {\n        // Toggle OFF: Hide current layer\n        this.hideLayer();\n        // Button-States werden von Grid-Komponenten verwaltet\n      } else {\n        // Toggle ON: Show new layer (or switch to different)\n        await this.displayLayer(kommune, categorySlug);\n        // Button-States werden von Grid-Komponenten verwaltet\n      }\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"[WFS] Toggle failed:\", error);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Enhanced displayLayer with state management\n   */\n  async displayLayer(\n    kommune: KommuneData,\n    categorySlug: string,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      // Hide existing layer but keep cached\n      if (this.activeLayer) {\n        this.activeLayer.setVisible(false);\n      }\n\n      // Button-States werden von Grid-Komponenten verwaltet\n\n      const config = this.buildLayerConfig(kommune, categorySlug);\n      const cacheKey = `${config.wpName}-${config.containerType}-${config.osmAdminLevel}`;\n\n      // Try to get from cache first\n      let layer = this.layerCache.get(cacheKey);\n      if (!layer) {\n        console.log(\"[WFS] Creating new layer for:\", cacheKey);\n        layer = await this.createWFSLayer(config);\n        this.layerCache.set(cacheKey, layer);\n        this.map.addLayer(layer);\n      } else {\n        console.log(\"[WFS] Reusing cached layer for:\", cacheKey);\n      }\n\n      this.activeLayer = layer;\n\n      // Show layer\n      this.activeLayer.setVisible(true);\n\n      // Update state\n      this.currentState = { kommune, categorySlug };\n\n      console.log(\n        `[WFS] Layer displayed: ${config.wpName} - ${config.containerType}`,\n      );\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"[WFS] Failed to display layer:\", error);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Enhanced hideLayer with state management\n   */\n  hideLayer(): void {\n    if (this.activeLayer) {\n      this.activeLayer.setVisible(false);\n      this.currentState = { kommune: null, categorySlug: null };\n      // Button-States werden von Grid-Komponenten verwaltet\n      console.log(\"[WFS] Layer hidden\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Build WFS layer configuration from kommune and category\n   */\n  private buildLayerConfig(\n    kommune: KommuneData,\n    categorySlug: string,\n  ): WFSLayerConfig {\n    const containerType = this.getContainerType(categorySlug);\n    const osmAdminLevel = this.getOsmAdminLevel(kommune, containerType);\n\n    return {\n      wpName: kommune.wp_name,\n      containerType,\n      osmAdminLevel,\n    };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Map category slug to container type\n   */\n  private getContainerType(categorySlug: string): string {\n    // Try to get from embedded client-side category data first\n    try {\n      const categoryDataElement = document.getElementById(\"category-data\");\n      if (categoryDataElement) {\n        const categoryMapStr =\n          categoryDataElement.getAttribute(\"data-category-map\");\n        if (categoryMapStr) {\n          const categoryMap = JSON.parse(categoryMapStr);\n          if (categoryMap[categorySlug]?.containerType) {\n            return categoryMap[categorySlug].containerType;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"[WFS] Failed to load category data from HTML:\", error);\n    }\n\n    // Fehler werfen statt Fallback, wenn containerType nicht gefunden\n    const errorMsg =\n      `[WFS] No containerType found for category '${categorySlug}'. ` +\n      `Make sure the category exists in src/content/kategorien/ ` +\n      `and has a 'containerType' field in its frontmatter.`;\n    console.error(errorMsg);\n    throw new Error(errorMsg);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Determine OSM admin level based on kommune and container type\n   */\n  private getOsmAdminLevel(\n    kommune: KommuneData,\n    containerType: string,\n  ): number {\n    if (containerType === \"cemetery\") {\n      return 8; // Friedhöfe immer Level 8\n    }\n\n    if (containerType === \"administrative\") {\n      const levels = kommune.osmAdminLevels || [];\n\n      // Verbesserte OSM Admin Level Logik für administrative Ebenen\n      console.log(\"[WFS] OSM Admin Levels for\", kommune.slug, \":\", levels);\n\n      // Regel: Nimm die NÄCHSTE Ebene nach der Kommune-Grenze\n      if (levels.length > 1) {\n        // Zweites Element = nächste Untergliederung nach Kommune-Grenze\n        const nextLevel = levels[1];\n        console.log(\n          \"[WFS] Using next admin level:\",\n          nextLevel,\n          \"from levels:\",\n          levels,\n        );\n        return nextLevel;\n      } else if (levels.length === 1) {\n        // Einzige verfügbare Ebene (Sonderfall wie Stadtstaaten)\n        const onlyLevel = levels[0];\n        console.log(\"[WFS] Using only available admin level:\", onlyLevel);\n        return onlyLevel;\n      } else {\n        console.warn(\n          \"[WFS] No OSM admin levels available for\",\n          kommune.slug,\n          \"using fallback 8\",\n        );\n      }\n    }\n\n    return 8; // Fallback\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create OpenLayers WFS VectorLayer\n   */\n  private async createWFSLayer(\n    config: WFSLayerConfig,\n  ): Promise<VectorLayer<VectorSource>> {\n    console.log(\"[WFS] WFS request with:\", {\n      wp_name: config.wpName,\n      containertype: config.containerType,\n      osmadminlevel: config.osmAdminLevel,\n    });\n\n    // Create properly encoded CQL filter - wp_name should NOT be double-encoded\n    const cqlFilter = `wp_name='${config.wpName}' AND container_type='${config.containerType}' AND osm_admin_level=${config.osmAdminLevel}`;\n\n    const wfsUrl = wfsAuthClient.buildAuthorizedWFSURL(\"p2d2_containers\", {\n      CQL_FILTER: cqlFilter,\n    });\n\n    // Create vector layer\n    const layer = new VectorLayer({\n      source: new VectorSource({\n        url: wfsUrl,\n        format: new GeoJSON(),\n      }),\n      style: new Style({\n        stroke: new Stroke({\n          color: \"#FF6900\",\n          width: 2,\n        }),\n        fill: new Fill({\n          color: \"rgba(255, 105, 0, 0.1)\",\n        }),\n      }),\n      visible: true,\n    });\n\n    return layer;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check if selection is same as current\n   */\n  private isSameSelection(kommune: KommuneData, categorySlug: string): boolean {\n    return (\n      this.currentState.kommune?.slug === kommune.slug &&\n      this.currentState.categorySlug === categorySlug\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get current active layer (for debugging)\n   */\n  getActiveLayer(): VectorLayer<VectorSource> | null {\n    return this.activeLayer;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check if layer is currently displayed\n   */\n  hasActiveLayer(): boolean {\n    return this.activeLayer !== null;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get current state (for debugging)\n   */\n  getCurrentState() {\n    return {\n      ...this.currentState,\n      hasActiveLayer: this.hasActiveLayer(),\n      isVisible: this.activeLayer?.getVisible() || false,\n    };\n  }\n}\n\nexport default WFSLayerManager;\n"

    "content": "/**\n * Tab State Persistence Utilities\n * Manages selected tab state across page reloads\n */\n\nconst TAB_STORAGE_KEY = \"p2d2_selected_tab\";\n\nexport type TabType = \"kommunen\" | \"kategorien\";\n\n/**\n * Get persisted tab selection from localStorage\n */\nexport function getPersistedTab(): TabType | null {\n  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") return null;\n\n  try {\n    const saved = localStorage.getItem(TAB_STORAGE_KEY);\n    if (saved === \"kommunen\" || saved === \"kategorien\") {\n      return saved as TabType;\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn(\"[tab-persistence] Could not read persisted tab:\", error);\n  }\n\n  return null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Persist tab selection to localStorage\n */\nexport function setPersistedTab(tab: TabType): void {\n  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") return;\n\n  try {\n    localStorage.setItem(TAB_STORAGE_KEY, tab);\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn(\"[tab-persistence] Could not persist tab:\", error);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Clear persisted tab selection\n */\nexport function clearPersistedTab(): void {\n  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") return;\n\n  try {\n    localStorage.removeItem(TAB_STORAGE_KEY);\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn(\"[tab-persistence] Could not clear persisted tab:\", error);\n  }\n}\n"

    "content": "import proj4 from \"proj4\";\nimport type { Map } from \"ol\";\nimport { register } from \"ol/proj/proj4\";\nimport { getPointResolution, transform, transformExtent } from \"ol/proj\";\nimport View from \"ol/View\";\n\n// Default projections\nexport const defaultCRS = \"EPSG:3857\";\nexport const wgs84 = \"EPSG:4326\";\n\n// Predefined ETRS89 UTM projections\nconst predefinedUtmDefs: Record<string, string> = {\n  \"EPSG:25832\":\n    \"+proj=utm +zone=32 +ellps=GRS80 +towgs84=0,0,0,0,0,0,0 +units=m +no_defs\",\n  \"EPSG:25833\":\n    \"+proj=utm +zone=33 +ellps=GRS80 +towgs84=0,0,0,0,0,0,0 +units=m +no_defs\",\n};\n\n// Track registered projections to avoid duplicate registrations\nconst registeredProjections = new Set<string>();\n\n/**\n * Validate WGS84 coordinate [longitude, latitude]\n */\nexport function isValidWgs84Coordinate(coord: any): boolean {\n  return (\n    Array.isArray(coord) &&\n    coord.length === 2 &&\n    coord.every(Number.isFinite) &&\n    coord[0] >= -180 &&\n    coord[0] <= 180 &&\n    coord[1] >= -90 &&\n    coord[1] <= 90\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Validate WGS84 extent [minLon, minLat, maxLon, maxLat]\n */\nexport function isValidWgs84Extent(extent: any): boolean {\n  return (\n    Array.isArray(extent) &&\n    extent.length === 4 &&\n    extent.every(Number.isFinite) &&\n    extent[0] >= -180 &&\n    extent[0] <= 180 &&\n    extent[1] >= -90 &&\n    extent[1] <= 90 &&\n    extent[2] >= -180 &&\n    extent[2] <= 180 &&\n    extent[3] >= -90 &&\n    extent[3] <= 90 &&\n    extent[0] <= extent[2] &&\n    extent[1] <= extent[3]\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a CRS is a UTM projection\n */\nexport function isUtm(crs: string): boolean {\n  return /^EPSG:(326\\d{2}|327\\d{2}|258\\d{2})$/.test(crs);\n}\n\n/**\n * Register a UTM projection dynamically\n */\nexport function registerUtm(crs: string): boolean {\n  if (registeredProjections.has(crs)) {\n    return true; // Already registered\n  }\n\n  // Check if it's a predefined UTM\n  if (predefinedUtmDefs[crs]) {\n    proj4.defs(crs, predefinedUtmDefs[crs]);\n    registeredProjections.add(crs);\n    register(proj4);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  // Handle dynamic UTM zones\n  const utmMatch = crs.match(/^EPSG:(326|327|258)(\\d{2})$/);\n  if (utmMatch) {\n    const [, prefix, zoneStr] = utmMatch;\n    const zone = parseInt(zoneStr, 10);\n\n    if (zone >= 1 && zone <= 60) {\n      const hemisphere = prefix === \"327\" ? \"+south\" : \"\";\n      const ellps =\n        prefix === \"258\"\n          ? \"+ellps=GRS80 +towgs84=0,0,0,0,0,0,0\"\n          : \"+ellps=WGS84\";\n\n      const def = `+proj=utm +zone=${zone} ${hemisphere} ${ellps} +units=m +no_defs`;\n      proj4.defs(crs, def);\n      registeredProjections.add(crs);\n      register(proj4);\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return false;\n}\n\n/**\n * Switch to a new view while preserving scale and rotation\n */\nexport function toNewViewPreservingScale(\n  map: Map,\n  targetEpsg: string,\n  animate: boolean = true,\n): boolean {\n  const view = map?.getView?.();\n  if (!view || !view.getProjection) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] map view is not initialized\");\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  const currentProj = view.getProjection();\n  if (!currentProj) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] current projection not available\");\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  if (currentProj.getCode() === targetEpsg) {\n    return true; // Already in target projection\n  }\n\n  // Register target projection if it's UTM\n  if (isUtm(targetEpsg) && !registerUtm(targetEpsg)) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] failed to register UTM projection\", targetEpsg);\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const currentCenter = view.getCenter();\n    const currentZoom = view.getZoom() || 10;\n    const currentRotation = view.getRotation();\n\n    if (!currentCenter) {\n      console.error(\"[crs] current center not available\");\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    // Simplified scale preservation: use current resolution directly\n    const currentResolution = view.getResolution();\n    let targetResolution = currentResolution;\n\n    // Apply simple scaling factor between different projection types\n    if (currentProj.getCode() === defaultCRS && isUtm(targetEpsg)) {\n      // Web Mercator to UTM: scale down slightly\n      targetResolution = currentResolution\n        ? currentResolution * 0.8\n        : undefined;\n    } else if (isUtm(currentProj.getCode()) && targetEpsg === defaultCRS) {\n      // UTM to Web Mercator: scale up slightly\n      targetResolution = currentResolution\n        ? currentResolution * 1.25\n        : undefined;\n    }\n\n    // Fallback to zoom-based approach if resolution calculation fails\n    if (\n      !targetResolution ||\n      !Number.isFinite(targetResolution) ||\n      targetResolution <= 0\n    ) {\n      console.warn(\"[crs] using zoom-based fallback\");\n      targetResolution = undefined;\n    }\n\n    // Transform center to target projection\n    let transformedCenter: number[] | undefined;\n    try {\n      transformedCenter = transform(currentCenter, currentProj, targetEpsg);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"[crs] failed to transform center\", error);\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!transformedCenter) {\n      console.error(\"[crs] transformed center is undefined\");\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    // Enhanced view creation with better scale preservation\n    const newView = new View({\n      projection: targetEpsg,\n      center: transformedCenter,\n      resolution: targetResolution || undefined,\n      zoom: targetResolution ? undefined : currentZoom,\n      rotation: currentRotation,\n      // Add constraints for better UX\n      maxZoom: 21,\n      minZoom: 1,\n    });\n\n    // Store old view reference for potential rollback\n    const oldView = map.getView();\n\n    map.setView(newView);\n\n    // Verify view change was successful\n    const actualNewView = map.getView();\n    if (\n      !actualNewView ||\n      actualNewView.getProjection()?.getCode() !== targetEpsg\n    ) {\n      console.error(\n        `[crs] View change verification failed. Expected: ${targetEpsg}, Got: ${actualNewView?.getProjection()?.getCode()}`,\n      );\n      // Rollback on failure\n      map.setView(oldView);\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    // Optional animation with error handling\n    if (animate) {\n      try {\n        actualNewView.animate({\n          center: actualNewView.getCenter(),\n          resolution: actualNewView.getResolution(),\n          rotation: actualNewView.getRotation(),\n          duration: 250,\n        });\n      } catch (animationError) {\n        console.warn(\n          \"[crs] Animation failed, but view change successful:\",\n          animationError,\n        );\n        // Animation failure doesn't affect the core functionality\n      }\n    }\n\n    console.log(`[crs] Successfully switched to projection: ${targetEpsg}`);\n    return true;\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] error in view preservation\", error);\n\n    // Additional error context\n    console.error(\"[crs] Error context:\", {\n      currentProj: currentProj?.getCode(),\n      targetEpsg,\n      currentCenter,\n      currentZoom,\n    });\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Transform extent from WGS84 to target projection\n * Enhanced with better error handling and validation\n */\n\n/**\n * Transform extent from WGS84 to target projection\n */\nexport function transformExtentFromWgs84(\n  extent: number[],\n  targetEpsg: string,\n): number[] | null {\n  // Input validation\n  if (!extent || !Array.isArray(extent) || extent.length !== 4) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] Invalid extent for transformation:\", extent);\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  if (!extent.every(Number.isFinite)) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] Extent contains non-finite values:\", extent);\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const result = transformExtent(extent, wgs84, targetEpsg);\n\n    // Validate result\n    if (\n      !result ||\n      !Array.isArray(result) ||\n      result.length !== 4 ||\n      !result.every(Number.isFinite)\n    ) {\n      console.error(\"[crs] Invalid transformation result:\", result);\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    return result;\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] failed to transform extent\", error);\n    console.error(\"[crs] Transform extent context:\", { extent, targetEpsg });\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Transform center from WGS84 to target projection\n */\nexport function transformCenterFromWgs84(\n  center: number[],\n  targetEpsg: string,\n): number[] | null {\n  // Input validation\n  if (!center || !Array.isArray(center) || center.length !== 2) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] Invalid center for transformation:\", center);\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  if (!center.every(Number.isFinite)) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] Center contains non-finite values:\", center);\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const result = transform(center, wgs84, targetEpsg);\n\n    // Validate result\n    if (\n      !result ||\n      !Array.isArray(result) ||\n      result.length !== 2 ||\n      !result.every(Number.isFinite)\n    ) {\n      console.error(\"[crs] Invalid transformation result:\", result);\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    return result;\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"[crs] failed to transform center\", error);\n    console.error(\"[crs] Transform center context:\", { center, targetEpsg });\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n// Pre-register common UTM projections on module load\nObject.keys(predefinedUtmDefs).forEach((crs) => {\n  registerUtm(crs);\n});\n"

    "name": "src/utils/utm-resolutions.ts",
    "content": "/**\n * UTM Projection Resolutions Utility\n *\n * Provides correct resolutions for ALL UTM projections (EPSG:25832, EPSG:25833, etc.)\n * to avoid OpenLayers fallback to EPSG:3857 (Mercator) resolutions.\n *\n * @module utm-resolutions\n */\n\n/**\n * Calculate resolutions array for UTM projections\n *\n * UNIVERSAL: Works for all 60 UTM zones (Zone 1-60, North/South)\n * All UTM zones use identical Transverse Mercator parameters.\n *\n * @param maxResolution - Maximum resolution at zoom 0 (meters per pixel). Default: 15000\n * @param maxZoom - Maximum zoom level. Default: 28\n * @returns Array of resolutions for each zoom level\n *\n * @example\n * ```\n * // For EPSG:25832 (Köln), EPSG:25833 (Berlin), EPSG:32610 (San Francisco), etc.\n * const resolutions = calculateUtmResolutions();\n *\n * const map = new OLMap({\n *   view: new View({\n *     projection: 'EPSG:25832',\n *     resolutions: resolutions,\n *     constrainResolution: false\n *   })\n * });\n * ```\n */\nexport function calculateUtmResolutions(\n  maxResolution: number = 15000,\n  maxZoom: number = 28\n): number[] {\n  const resolutions: number[] = [];\n\n  for (let z = 0; z <= maxZoom; z++) {\n    resolutions.push(maxResolution / Math.pow(2, z));\n  }\n\n  return resolutions;\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a projection is a UTM projection\n *\n * @param projection - EPSG code (e.g., \"EPSG:25832\")\n * @returns true if projection is UTM\n */\nexport function isUtmProjection(projection: string): boolean {\n  // EPSG:32601-32660 (WGS84 UTM North)\n  // EPSG:32701-32760 (WGS84 UTM South)\n  // EPSG:25828-25838 (ETRS89 UTM Zones 28N-38N)\n  return /^EPSG:(326[0-9]{2}|327[0-9]{2}|258[2-3][0-9])$/.test(projection);\n}\n\n/**\n * Get recommended maxResolution for a projection\n *\n * @param projection - EPSG code\n * @returns Recommended maxResolution in meters per pixel\n */\nexport function getRecommendedMaxResolution(projection: string): number {\n  if (isUtmProjection(projection)) {\n    return 15000;  // ~15km/px at zoom 0 for all UTM zones\n  }\n\n  // EPSG:3857 (Web Mercator)\n  if (projection === 'EPSG:3857') {\n    return 156543.03;  // Standard Web Mercator\n  }\n\n  // Fallback\n  return 15000;\n}\n"

    "name": "src/utils/logger.ts",
    "content": "import type { AstroIntegrationLogger } from \"astro\";\n\n// Simple logger that can work with Astro's logger or fallback to console\n// Note: AstroIntegrationLogger is only available during integration runtime\nexport interface Logger {\n  info(message: string, data?: any): void;\n  warn(message: string, data?: any): void;\n  error(message: string, error?: Error | string, data?: any): void;\n  debug(message: string, data?: any): void;\n}\n\n// Default console logger for when Astro logger is not available\nconst consoleLogger: Logger = {\n  info: (message: string, data?: any) => {\n    console.log(`[INFO] ${message}`, data || \"\");\n  },\n  warn: (message: string, data?: any) => {\n    console.warn(`[WARN] ${message}`, data || \"\");\n  },\n  error: (message: string, error?: Error | string, data?: any) => {\n    console.error(`[ERROR] ${message}`, error || \"\", data || \"\");\n  },\n  debug: (message: string, data?: any) => {\n    if (process.env.DEBUG) {\n      console.debug(`[DEBUG] ${message}`, data || \"\");\n    }\n  },\n};\n\n// Create a logger that can adapt to Astro's integration logger or use console\nexport function createLogger(astroLogger?: AstroIntegrationLogger): Logger {\n  if (astroLogger) {\n    return {\n      info: (message: string, data?: any) =>\n        astroLogger.info(message + (data ? ` ${JSON.stringify(data)}` : \"\")),\n      warn: (message: string, data?: any) =>\n        astroLogger.warn(message + (data ? ` ${JSON.stringify(data)}` : \"\")),\n      error: (message: string, error?: Error | string, data?: any) => {\n        const errorMsg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : error;\n        astroLogger.error(\n          `${message}: ${errorMsg}` + (data ? ` ${JSON.stringify(data)}` : \"\"),\n        );\n      },\n      debug: (message: string, data?: any) => {\n        if (process.env.DEBUG) {\n          astroLogger.info(\n            `[DEBUG] ${message}` + (data ? ` ${JSON.stringify(data)}` : \"\"),\n          );\n        }\n      },\n    };\n  }\n\n  return consoleLogger;\n}\n\n// Global logger instance\nexport const logger: Logger = consoleLogger;\n\n// Helper to set Astro logger context\nexport function setAstroLogger(astroLogger: AstroIntegrationLogger): void {\n  const astroBasedLogger = createLogger(astroLogger);\n\n  // Override global logger methods\n  logger.info = astroBasedLogger.info;\n  logger.warn = astroBasedLogger.warn;\n  logger.error = astroBasedLogger.error;\n  logger.debug = astroBasedLogger.debug;\n}\n"

    "name": "src/utils/wfs-auth.ts",
    "content": "/**\n * WFS Authorization Client\n * Handles authentication and authorized requests to WFS endpoints\n */\n\nexport interface WFSCredentials {\n  username: string;\n  password: string;\n}\n\nexport interface WFSConfig {\n  endpoint: string;\n  workspace: string;\n  namespace: string;\n  credentials: WFSCredentials;\n}\n\n// Environment detection type\ninterface EnvironmentInfo {\n  isDev: boolean;\n  nodeEnv: string | undefined;\n  hostname: string | undefined;\n}\n\nfunction detectEnvironment(): EnvironmentInfo {\n  return {\n    isDev:\n      (typeof process !== \"undefined\" &&\n        process.env.NODE_ENV === \"development\") ||\n      (typeof window !== \"undefined\" &&\n        window.location.hostname.includes(\"local\")),\n    nodeEnv: typeof process !== \"undefined\" ? process.env.NODE_ENV : undefined,\n    hostname:\n      typeof window !== \"undefined\" ? window.location.hostname : undefined,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * WFS Authorization Client\n * Handles authentication and authorized requests to WFS endpoints\n */\n\nexport interface WFSCredentials {\n  username: string;\n  password: string;\n}\n\nexport interface WFSConfig {\n  endpoint: string;\n  workspace: string;\n  namespace: string;\n  credentials: WFSCredentials;\n}\n\nexport class WFSAuthClient {\n  private config: WFSConfig;\n\n  constructor(config: Partial<WFSConfig> = {}) {\n    // Environment detection using unified function\n    const envInfo = detectEnvironment();\n    const isDev = envInfo.isDev;\n\n    // Environment-spezifische Defaults\n    // Weiche für dev/prod ohne import.meta.env Abhängigkeit\n\n    // Für Development: Versuche zuerst globalen Endpoint\n    const defaultEndpoint = isDev\n      ? \"https://wfs.data-dna.eu/geoserver/ows\" // Development mit CORS-fähigem Endpoint\n      : \"https://wfs.data-dna.eu/geoserver/Verwaltungsdaten/ows\"; // Production Endpoint\n\n    // TODO: Issue https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2/-/issues/1 - Remove hardcoded credentials once anonymous GeoServer access is configured\n    // These are temporary read-only credentials that will be replaced with anonymous access\n    const RO_USERNAME = \"p2d2_wfs_user\"; // Echter Read-Only User\n    const RO_PASSWORD = \"eif1nu4ao9Loh0oobeev\"; // Echtes Read-Only Passwort\n\n    // Log warning in development about temporary credentials\n    if (isDev && RO_USERNAME === \"p2d2_wfs_user\") {\n      console.warn(\n        \"[WFS-Auth] Using temporary hardcoded read-only credentials. See Issue https://gitlab.opencode.de/OC000028072444/p2d2/-/issues/1 for roadmap to anonymous access.\",\n      );\n    }\n\n    this.config = {\n      endpoint: config.endpoint || defaultEndpoint,\n      workspace: config.workspace || \"Verwaltungsdaten\",\n      namespace: config.namespace || \"urn:data-dna:govdata\",\n      credentials: {\n        username: config.credentials?.username || RO_USERNAME,\n        password: config.credentials?.password || RO_PASSWORD,\n      },\n    };\n\n    if (isDev) {\n      console.log(\"[WFS-Auth] Configuration loaded:\", {\n        endpoint: this.config.endpoint,\n        workspace: this.config.workspace,\n        namespace: this.config.namespace,\n        hasUsername: !!this.config.credentials.username,\n        hasPassword: !!this.config.credentials.password,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Validierung der kritischen Konfiguration\n    if (!this.config.endpoint) {\n      throw new Error(\"[WFS-Auth] Endpoint configuration missing\");\n    }\n\n    // Enhanced logging for debugging\n    if (isDev) {\n      console.log(\"[WFS-Auth] Configuration loaded:\", {\n        endpoint: this.config.endpoint,\n        workspace: this.config.workspace,\n        hasCredentials: !!(\n          this.config.credentials.username && this.config.credentials.password\n        ),\n        usingProxy: true,\n        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Warnung wenn Credentials fehlen\n    if (\n      !this.config.credentials.username ||\n      !this.config.credentials.password\n    ) {\n      console.warn(\"[WFS-Auth] Missing credentials - requests may fail\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ... Rest der Methoden bleibt unverändert ...\n\n  /**\n   * Validates if the configured workspace exists in GeoServer\n   */\n  async validateWorkspace(): Promise<boolean> {\n    try {\n      const capabilitiesUrl = `${this.config.endpoint.replace(\"/ows\", \"/wfs\")}?service=WFS&version=2.0.0&request=GetCapabilities`;\n      const response = await fetch(capabilitiesUrl);\n      const text = await response.text();\n\n      // Prüfe ob Workspace existiert\n      const hasWorkspace =\n        text.includes(`xmlns:${this.config.workspace}=`) ||\n        text.includes(`<Name>${this.config.workspace}:`);\n\n      console.log(\n        `[WFS-Auth] Workspace '${this.config.workspace}' ${hasWorkspace ? \"found\" : \"NOT FOUND\"} in capabilities`,\n      );\n      return hasWorkspace;\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"[WFS-Auth] Workspace validation failed:\", error);\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Builds a WFS GetFeature URL with authorization parameters\n   */\n  buildAuthorizedWFSURL(\n    typeName: string,\n    params: Record<string, string> = {},\n  ): string {\n    // Erlaubte Parameter definieren\n    const allowedParams = [\n      \"bbox\",\n      \"maxFeatures\",\n      \"CQL_FILTER\",\n      \"propertyName\",\n      \"sortBy\",\n    ];\n    const safeParams = Object.fromEntries(\n      Object.entries(params).filter(([key]) => allowedParams.includes(key)),\n    );\n\n    // Erkennung ob workspace-spezifischer oder globaler Endpoint\n    const useGlobalEndpoint =\n      this.config.endpoint.includes(\"/ows\") &&\n      !this.config.endpoint.includes(`/${this.config.workspace}/ows`);\n\n    const baseParams = {\n      service: \"WFS\",\n      version: \"2.0.0\", // Upgrade auf WFS 2.0.0 für bessere Namespace-Behandlung\n      request: \"GetFeature\",\n      typeName: useGlobalEndpoint\n        ? `${this.config.workspace}:${typeName}`\n        : typeName, // Bei workspace-spezifischem Endpoint ohne Prefix\n      outputFormat: \"application/json\", // Oder \"text/xml\" falls JSON nicht funktioniert\n      srsName: \"EPSG:4326\",\n      ...safeParams,\n    };\n\n    // Manueller URL-Bau um doppeltes Encoding zu vermeiden\n    const queryString = Object.entries(baseParams)\n      .map(([key, value]) => `${key}=${encodeURIComponent(String(value))}`)\n      .join(\"&\");\n\n    const wfsUrl = `${this.config.endpoint}?${queryString}`;\n\n    // Proxy-URL für CORS-Umgehung verwenden\n    const encodedWfsUrl = encodeURIComponent(wfsUrl);\n    const proxyUrl = `/api/wfs-proxy?url=${encodedWfsUrl}`;\n\n    console.log(`[WFS-Auth] Built Proxy URL: ${proxyUrl}`);\n    return proxyUrl;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Builds HTTP headers with authentication\n   */\n  private buildHeaders(existingHeaders?: HeadersInit): Headers {\n    const headers = new Headers(existingHeaders);\n\n    // Add Basic Auth only if valid credentials are provided\n    if (\n      this.config.credentials.username?.trim() &&\n      this.config.credentials.password?.trim() &&\n      this.config.credentials.username !== \"\" &&\n      this.config.credentials.password !== \"\"\n    ) {\n      const authString = btoa(\n        `${this.config.credentials.username}:${this.config.credentials.password}`,\n      );\n      headers.set(\"Authorization\", `Basic ${authString}`);\n    }\n\n    return headers;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Builds a WFS GetFeature URL with BBOX filter\n   */\n  buildBBoxWFSURL(\n    typeName: string,\n    bbox: number[],\n    crs: string = \"EPSG:4326\",\n  ): string {\n    const [minx, miny, maxx, maxy] = bbox;\n    return this.buildAuthorizedWFSURL(typeName, {\n      bbox: `${minx},${miny},${maxx},${maxy},${crs}`,\n      srsName: crs,\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Fetches data from WFS endpoint with authentication\n   */\n  async fetchWithAuth(\n    url: string,\n    options: RequestInit = {},\n  ): Promise<Response> {\n    try {\n      // Für Proxy-Requests keine zusätzlichen Headers benötigt\n      const isProxyRequest = url.startsWith(\"/api/wfs-proxy\");\n\n      const response = await fetch(url, {\n        ...options,\n        headers: isProxyRequest\n          ? new Headers(options.headers)\n          : this.buildHeaders(options.headers),\n        credentials: \"include\" as RequestCredentials,\n      });\n\n      if (!response.ok) {\n        const text = await response.text();\n\n        // Spezielle Behandlung für Namespace-Fehler (nur bei direkten WFS-Requests)\n        if (!isProxyRequest && text.includes(\"Unknown namespace\")) {\n          console.warn(\n            `[WFS-Auth] Namespace error detected, trying alternative endpoint...`,\n          );\n\n          // Fallback: Globaler Endpoint\n          const fallbackUrl = url.replace(\n            \"/geoserver/Verwaltungsdaten/ows\",\n            \"/geoserver/ows\",\n          );\n\n          if (fallbackUrl !== url) {\n            console.log(\n              `[WFS-Auth] Retrying with global endpoint: ${fallbackUrl}`,\n            );\n            return await fetch(fallbackUrl, {\n              ...options,\n              headers: this.buildHeaders(options.headers),\n              credentials: \"include\" as RequestCredentials,\n            });\n          }\n        }\n\n        throw new Error(\n          `WFS request failed: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}\\n${text}`,\n        );\n      }\n\n      return response;\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"[WFS-Auth] Request failed:\", error);\n      throw error;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Fetches GeoJSON features from WFS\n   */\n  async getFeatures(\n    typeName: string,\n    params: Record<string, string> = {},\n  ): Promise<any> {\n    const url = this.buildAuthorizedWFSURL(typeName, params);\n    const response = await this.fetchWithAuth(url);\n    return response.json();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Fetches features within a bounding box\n   */\n  async getFeaturesInBBox(\n    typeName: string,\n    bbox: number[],\n    crs: string = \"EPSG:4326\",\n  ): Promise<any> {\n    const url = this.buildBBoxWFSURL(typeName, bbox, crs);\n    const response = await this.fetchWithAuth(url);\n    return response.json();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Executes a WFS-T transaction\n   */\n  async executeWFSTransaction(transactionXml: string): Promise<Response> {\n    const headers = new Headers({\n      \"Content-Type\": \"application/xml\",\n    });\n\n    if (this.config.credentials.username && this.config.credentials.password) {\n      const authString = btoa(\n        `${this.config.credentials.username}:${this.config.credentials.password}`,\n      );\n      headers.set(\"Authorization\", `Basic ${authString}`);\n    }\n\n    const response = await fetch(this.config.endpoint, {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers,\n      body: transactionXml,\n      credentials: \"include\" as RequestCredentials,\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `WFS-T transaction failed: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Checks if WFS endpoint is accessible\n   */\n  async testConnection(): Promise<boolean> {\n    try {\n      const testUrl = this.buildAuthorizedWFSURL(\"p2d2_containers\", {\n        maxFeatures: \"1\",\n      });\n      const response = await this.fetchWithAuth(testUrl, { method: \"HEAD\" });\n      return response.ok;\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\"WFS connection test failed:\", error);\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Gets the current configuration\n   */\n  getConfig(): Readonly<WFSConfig> {\n    return { ...this.config };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create WFS-T client with write credentials from import.meta.env\n   */\n  static createWFSTClient(): WFSAuthClient {\n    return new WFSAuthClient({\n      endpoint:\n        import.meta.env.WFST_ENDPOINT ||\n        \"https://wfs.data-dna.eu/geoserver/ows\",\n      workspace: import.meta.env.WFST_WORKSPACE || \"Verwaltungsdaten\",\n      namespace: import.meta.env.WFST_NAMESPACE || \"urn:data-dna:govdata\",\n      credentials: {\n        username: import.meta.env.WFST_USERNAME || \"\",\n        password: import.meta.env.WFST_PASSWORD || \"\",\n      },\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// Export singleton instance - sollte explizit mit Konfiguration erstellt werden\nexport const wfsAuthClient = new WFSAuthClient();\n"

toggleBaseLayer(layerName: string): void {\n  if (layerName === \"luftbild\" && luftbildLayer) {\n    // Toggle Luftbild unabhängig\n    const newVisibility = !luftbildLayer.getVisible();\n    luftbildLayer.setVisible(newVisibility);\n\n    // Update nur den Luftbild-Button\n    const luftbildBtn = document.querySelector(\n      '[data-layer-toggle=\"luftbild\"]',\n    );\n    if String(basemapLayer ? basemapLayer.getVisible() : false),\n    );\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn(\"Could not persist layer states\", error);\n  }\n\n  console.log(\"Layer toggle:\", {\n }\n  });\n\n  // Restore previous layer states from localStorage\n  try {\n    const luftbildVisible = localStorage.getItem(\"luftbildVisible\") === \"true\";\n    const basemapVisible = localStorage.getItem(\"basemapVisible\") === \"true\";\n\n    if (luftbildVisible && luftbildLayer) {\n      luftbildLayer.setVisible(true);\n      const btn = LayerInteractionState> = new Map();\n  private readonly LONG_PRESS_THRESHOLD = 500; // ms\n  private readonly OPACITY_CYCLE_DURATION = 12000; // ms\n  private readonly MIN_OPACITY = 0.2;\n  private   * @param layerId - Unique layer identifier for localStorage (e.g., \"luftbild\")\n   */\n  registerLongPress(\n    buttonId: string,\n    layer: TileLayer<any>,\n    layerId: string,\n  ): void {\n    const button = document.getElementById(buttonId);\n    if (!button) {\n      console.warn(`[LayerInteraction] Button ${buttonId} not found`);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize state\n    const state: LayerInteractionState = {\n      isPressed: false,\n      pressStartTime: 0,\n      animationFrameId: null,\n      initialOpacity: layer.getOpacity(),\n      currentOpacity: layer.getOpacity(),\n      wasLongPress: false,\n    };\n    this.states.set(buttonId, state);\n\n    // Restore saved opacity from localStorage\n    this.restoreOpacity(layer, layerId, state);\n\n    // === MOUSE EVENTS ===\n    const handleMouseDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault(); // Prevent default (text selection, drag)\n      this.startPressTracking(state, layer, buttonId, layerId);\n    };\n\n    const handleMouseUp = () => {\n      this.stopPressTracking(state, layer, layerId);\n    };\n\n    const handleMouseLeave = () => {\n      this.stopPressTracking(state, layer, layerId);\n    };\n\n    button.addEventListener(\"mousedown\", handleMouseDown);\n    button.addEventListener(\"mouseup\", handleMouseUp);\n    button.addEventListener(\"mouseleave\", handleMouseLeave);\n\n    // === TOUCH EVENTS ===\n    const handleTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault(); // Prevent default touch behavior\n      this.startPressTracking(state, layer, buttonId, layerId);\n    };\n\n    const handleTouchEnd = () => {\n      this.stopPressTracking(state, layer, layerId);\n    };\n\n    const handleTouchCancel = () => {\n      this.stopPressTracking(state, layer, layerId);\n    };\n\n    button.addEventListener(\"touchstart\", handleTouchStart, { passive: false });\n    button.addEventListener(\"touchend\", handleTouchEnd);\n    button.addEventListener(\"touchcancel\", handleTouchCancel);\n\n    console.log(\n      `[LayerInteraction] Registered for ${buttonId} (Layer: ${layerId})`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Start press tracking and schedule animation\n   */\n  private startPressTracking(\n    state: LayerInteractionState,\n    layer: TileLayer<any>,\n    buttonId: string,\n    layerId: string,\n  ): void {\n    state.isPressed = true;\n    state.pressStartTime = Date.now();\n    state.initialOpacity = layer.getOpacity();\n\n    // Schedule animation after LONG_PRESS_THRESHOLD\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      if (state.isPressed) {\n        // Still pressed after threshold → start animation\n        state.wasLongPress = true;\n        this.startOpacityAnimation(buttonId, layer, state, layerId);\n      }\n    }, this.LONG_PRESS_THRESHOLD);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Stop press tracking and cleanup\n   */\n  private stopPressTracking(\n    state: LayerInteractionState,\n    layer: TileLayer<any>,\n    layerId: string,\n  ): void {\n    const pressDuration = Date.now() - state.pressStartTime;\n    state.isPressed = false;\n\n    if (state.animationFrameId !== null) {\n      // Animation was running → save final opacity\n      this.stopAnimation(state, layer, layerId);\n    } else if (pressDuration < this.LONG_PRESS_THRESHOLD) {\n      // Short press → do nothing (toggle handled by LayerControls)\n      // This is intentional: Short press = toggle, Long press = opacity adjustment\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Start sinusoidal opacity animation\n   */\n  private startOpacityAnimation(\n    buttonId: string,\n    layer: TileLayer<any>,\n    state: LayerInteractionState,\n    layerId: string,\n  ): void {\n    const startTime = Date.now();\n\n    const animate = (timestamp: number) => {\n      if (!state.isPressed) {\n        // Animation should stop\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Calculate elapsed time and progress\n      const elapsed = timestamp - startTime;\n      const progress =\n        (elapsed % this.OPACITY_CYCLE_DURATION) / this.OPACITY_CYCLE_DURATION;\n\n      // Sinusoidal oscillation: 0.2 → 1.0 → 0.2\n      // Formula: center + amplitude * sin(2π * progress)\n      const center = (this.MIN_OPACITY + this.MAX_OPACITY) / 2; // 0.6\n      const amplitude = (this.MAX_OPACITY - this.MIN_OPACITY) / 2; // 0.4\n      const opacity = center + amplitude * Math.sin(progress * Math.PI * 2);\n\n      state.currentOpacity = opacity;\n\n      // Update layer opacity (OpenLayers handles rendering automatically)\n      layer.setOpacity(opacity);\n\n      // Visual feedback: Button opacity follows layer\n      const button = document.getElementById(buttonId);\n      if (button) {\n        button.style.opacity = String(opacity);\n      }\n\n      // Schedule next frame\n      state.animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n    };\n\n    // Start animation loop\n    state.animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n    console.log(`[LayerInteraction] Animation started for ${layerId}`);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Stop animation and persist final opacity\n   */\n  private stopAnimation(\n    state: LayerInteractionState,\n    layer: TileLayer<any>,\n    layerId: string,\n  ): void {\n    // Cancel animation frame\n    if (state.animationFrameId !== null) {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(state.animationFrameId);\n      state.animationFrameId = null;\n    }\n\n    // Persist final opacity to localStorage\n    const finalOpacity = state.currentOpacity;\n    this.saveOpacity(layerId, finalOpacity);\n\n    // Reset button opacity\n    const buttonId = this.getButtonIdFromLayerId(layerId);\n    const button = document.getElementById(buttonId);\n    if (button) {\n      button.style.opacity = \"1\";\n    }\n\n    // Reset flag after short delay to prevent click event\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      state.wasLongPress = false;\n    }, 100);\n\n    console.log(\n      `[LayerInteraction] Saved opacity ${finalOpacity.toFixed(2)} for ${layerId}`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Restore opacity from localStorage\n   */\n  private restoreOpacity(\n    layer: TileLayer<any>,\n    layerId: string,\n    state: LayerInteractionState,\n  ): void {\n    try {\n      const saved = localStorage.getItem(`layer-opacity-${layerId}`);\n      if (saved) {\n        const opacity = parseFloat(saved);\n        if (opacity >= this.MIN_OPACITY && opacity <= this.MAX_OPACITY) {\n          layer.setOpacity(opacity);\n          state.initialOpacity = opacity;\n          state.currentOpacity = opacity;\n          console.log(\n            `[LayerInteraction] Restored opacity ${opacity.toFixed(2)} for ${layerId}`,\n          );\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\n        `[LayerInteraction] Failed to restore opacity for ${layerId}`,\n        error,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Save opacity to localStorage\n   */\n  private saveOpacity(layerId: string, opacity: number): void {\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(`layer-opacity-${layerId}`, String(opacity));\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\n        `[LayerInteraction] Failed to save opacity for ${layerId}`,\n        error,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Helper: Get button ID from layer ID\n   */\n  private getButtonIdFromLayerId(layerId: string): string {\n    return `toggle-${layerId}`;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Cleanup all animations (call on component unmount)\n   */\n  cleanup(): void {\n    this.states.forEach((state, buttonId) => {\n      if (state.animationFrameId !== null) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(state.animationFrameId);\n      }\n      const button = document.getElementById(buttonId);\n      if (button) {\n        button.style.opacity = \"1\";\n      }\n    });\n    this.states.clear();\n    console.log(\"[LayerInteraction] Cleanup completed\");\n  }\n}\n"

coord[1] >= -90 &&\n      coord[1] <= 90\n    );\n  }\n\n  private isValidExtent(\n    extent: any,\n  ): extent is [number, number, number, number] {\n    return (\n      Array.isArray(extent) &&\n private handleClick(event: Event): void {\n    const target = event.target as HTMLElement;\n\n    // KRITISCH: Native Links nicht blockieren!\n    if (target.closest(\"a[href]\")) return; // Native     // Neuen Button highlighten\n      button.classList.add(\"highlighted\");\n\n      console.log(\n        \"[kommunen-handler] Processing click for:\",\n        detail.slug,\n        detail,\n      );\n\n      // 1. NAVIGATION\n      this.dispatchKommunenFocus(detail);\n\n      // 2. WFS LAYER MANAGEMENT - NEU HINZUGEFÜGT!\n      this.handleWFSLayerToggle(detail);\n\n      // In mapState speichern\n      (window as any).mapState?.setSelectedKommune?.(detail);\n      console.log(\n console.error(\"[kommunen-handler] Click handler error:\", error);\n    } finally {\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.processingButtons.delete(button);\n      }, 500);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // NEW: Handle WFS layer switching on kommune change\n  private handleWFSLayerToggle(detail: KommunenDetail): void {\n    if (!(window as any).wfsManager) return;\n\n    const selectedCategory = (window as any).mapState?.getSelectedCategory?.();\n    if (!selectedCategory) {\n      console.log(\"[WFS] No category selected, skipping WFS layer toggle\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // CRITICAL: Always switch layer - this will handle hide old and show new\n    console.log(\n      \"[WFS] Auto-switching layer for kommune change to:\",\n      detail.slug,\n    );\n    (window as any).wfsManager.displayLayer(detail, selectedCategory);\n  }\n\n  private dispatchKommunenFocus(detail: KommunenDetail): void {\n    try {\n      if (typeof (window as any).dispatchKommunenFocus === CustomEvent(\"kommunen:focus\", { detail }));\n      } else {\n        console.error(\"[kommunen-handler] No event dispatch method available\");\n      }\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\"[kommunen-handler] Dispatch failed, retrying...\", error);\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(\"kommunen:focus\", { detail }));\n      }, 100);\n    }\n  }\n\n  private persistSelection(detail: KommunenDetail): void {\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(\"p2d2_selected_kommune_slug\", detail.slug || \"\");\n      localStorage.setItem(\n        \"p2d2_selected_kommune_detail\",\n        JSON.stringify(detail),\n      );\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\"[kommunen-handler] Could not persist selection:\", error);\n    }\n  }\n\n \"[kommunen-handler] Could not restore last selection:\",\n        error,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Get full kommune data from embedded content collection data\n  private getKommuneData(slug:
